- Computer: «This is no decision. I ask again, do you save your people or abandon them for the sake of the war? Attempting both will merely dilute your efforts. Would you trade one victory for two failures?» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]
- Computer: «And in so doing, alert the enemy to their broken codes. They would know that their weakness was exposed. They would reinforce, and the war would go on. You saved thousands at the expense of victory.» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]
- Computer: «Very good. If you had moved to evacuate the city, you would have alerted the enemy to their lost codes.» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]
- Computer: «You achieved the proper result with logic that does not match the pattern in memory. I shall adjust my evaluation.» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]
- Computer: «Hypothetical: remove the ongoing war from the previous example. Consider enemy states to be weak and remote. With no external threat, your empire stagnates. Your people become complacent and begin to question you. Same scenario as before; you discover an impending attack, but also a weakness that will come after. How do you react?» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]
- Computer: «No, you can't hide behind the greater morality. This is a single attack. The enemy does not intend a sustained conflict. There is no war to end. You must choose the outcome based only on its immediate benefits and consequences.» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]
- Computer: «But if nothing happens, your people will have no need of heroes. No, they don't see a threat. You have coddled them. Your empire will be brought down by introspection. You have failed to match the pattern in memory.» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]
- Computer: «Yes, you are ready. Soon you will recognize the proper course to follow. The Star Map is yours.» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]
- Computer: «That information is not available. Soon you will recognize the proper course to follow. The Star Map is yours.» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]
- Gorwooken: «I will take you directly to him. He is waiting in the throne room.» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]
- Freyyr: «Did he? I'm surprised it took him this long. Very well, do your worst!» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]
- Jolee: «Is that it? You spared his life initially just to cut him off before he can fully explain himself?» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]
- Freyyr: «To my shame, Zaalbar was exiled and enslaved. Do you dare claim to be my son's owner, outsider?» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]
- Freyyr: «Does he? Then he sees something of worth in you. I will listen... cautiously. Gullibility has harmed me in the past.» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]
- Freyyr: «I will answer, and then ask something of you.» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]
- Freyyr: «That would depend on you, {{s» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]
- Jolee: «Perhaps not. Do what you will. I'm just wondering if Chuundar is the wiser choice of ally.» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]
- Freyyr: «Many have tried to hunt this beast, but only a few who have seen it have lived to tell the tale. And none have ever managed to kill the creature. Many years ago, when I was far younger than I am now, a Jedi came to hunt the creature... a proud and boastful young man. Like many before him, he did not return. The beast is cunning. It will not appear if there is an overwhelming number waiting to slay it. But if you gather in small numbers, we know how to lure it out. Fresh blood will draw it out. Before Chuundar's rule put an end to the ritual, fresh kills were often left as offerings in the south of this area.» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]
- Freyyr: «My invisible attackers are a menagerie of my own fears and regrets, so many more than actual beasts that walk. No, I have not seen the creatures that you mention. I have been... preoccupied.» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]
- Freyyr: «Well, now you are just having fun with me. It's probably just some malfunctioning Czerka cast-off. Seriously though, if we are to challenge Chuundar based on tradition, I suggest you don't say these things to anyone. You risk contradicting our most ancient tales. Perhaps this warrants investigating, but not now.» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]
- Jolee: «Look, I'm not your nanny-droid, but I just don't think following Chuundar is the best course.» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]
- Freyyr: «I hope you can help. I must try to fix what has gone wrong here.» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]
- Freyyr: «You have? Please let me see it. I must be sure!» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]
- Freyyr: «What? I... I have nothing. I've been here for years. When we have succeeded, I will see you are rewarded.» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]
- Freyyr: «It... it is! It may not look like much... but it is a very important relic of my people. Tradition dictates that it be respected. I didn't think I was worthy to search for it, but I realize that was selfish despair. I should have challenged Chuundar long ago. I will make amends now. I have new hope. You have led me to this. Perhaps that is what the Great Beast wanted.» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]
- Freyyr: «I will climb to the surface as quickly as possible and try to gather support. You will have to follow on the paths as soon as you can, {{s» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]
- Basket: «[You begin the long ascent to the upper boughs of Kashyyyk.]» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]
- Chorrawl: «Freyyr went to the throne room to challenge Chuundar. I will take you to him now. It is the only way. I'm sorry, but I won't allow you to proceed any other way. This is too important.» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]
- Zaalbar: «Use it, of course. It has no more worth to my people. Not with Chuundar as chieftain. The old ways are dead. You showed me that. No, this sword will serve me as I see fit. It is no longer a symbol that anyone here recognizes. Still... maybe...» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]
- HK-47: «Observation: Your ruthlessness and cruelty are quite refreshing in an organic meatbag, master. Are you sure you aren't part droid?» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]
- Zaalbar: «Grrrgh... as you wish...» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]
- Grrrwahrr: «Please... try to help. Is there nothing... you can do? Please...» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]
- Canderous: «Mighty generous of you. A little too generous, if you ask me.» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Grrrwahrr: «I've never seen their kind before, not even among the Czerka. You saw them, armored head to toe, yet blending with the forest.» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]
- Grrrwahrr: «You have already fought some of them. How can you claim it is not your problem? They may come for you next, waiting until you put your weapons away, like they attacked us. I should have known not to expect much from an outsider. I will find them on my own... as weak as I am.» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]
- Grrrwahrr: «I thank you. I will wait here. I am too weak to fight them again, but I refuse to leave the Shadowlands unless vengeance is sated. Please, if you kill them, I will reward you with whatever I can. They are not worthy of life. Look for them where the bodies of my fellows are. They will only attack you if you are not armed. The cowards.» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]
- Mandalorian Commander: «You have interrupted our hunt, interloper. The inhabitants of this world could do little against us, but you appear to be a threat.» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]
- Grrrwahrr: «I thank you. These creatures, they have no honor. Let their blood feed the Shadowlands.» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]
- Grrrwahrr: «Wait... you have helped in avenging my fellows. I must acknowledge that, even though you are an outsider and are not to be truly trusted.» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]

**[K1/Manaan]**
- Canderous: «Water worlds never had much of an appeal for us to conquer. They may put up a good fight in their own environment - no match for us, of course - but they don't really have anything of value either. The thing this world is good for is a substance known as Kolto. Kolto is the single best healing substance in the galaxy. But the Mandalore are not as weak and fragile as your Republic and Sith warriors. When we enter battle, we carry weapons powerful enough to pulverize cities. Both in our wars, and in our own personal combats, there are no survivors. So Kolto does not hold the appeal it does for your peoples. You have anything else you want to ask?» [K1/Manaan]
- Mission: «Manaan's the source for all the kolto in the galaxy; that's why those fish-faced Selkath can stay neutral in this whole Sith-Republic war. But I don't know much else about this place. Sorry I can't be more help, you know? Is there anything else I can do for you?» [K1/Manaan]

**[K1/Matale_Grounds]**
- Canderous: «And don't think I wouldn't enjoy blowing you to bits.» [K1/Matale_Grounds]
- Zuulan's Speeder: «[You leave the speeder alone for now.]» [K1/Matale_Grounds]
- Zuulan's Speeder: «[Success: You set the fragmentation mine, then move far enough away so that you won't get caught in the blast when Zuulan shows up.]» [K1/Matale_Grounds]
- Zuulan Sentar: «Here - you take credits. Good deal. Me go now.» [K1/Matale_Grounds]
- Zuulan Sentar: «You think Zuulan let you take him to prison? You think Zuulan sit in cell and rot?» [K1/Matale_Grounds]

**[K1/Mission]**
- Mission: «Hey, there. What can I do for you?» [K1/Mission]
- Mission: «Really? You want to know about me? Nobody's ever really been interested in me before. What did you want to know?» [K1/Mission]
- Mission: «Big Z's my family, you know? My parents... well, I guess they're dead. It was just me on my own until the day I saw Zaalbar in the Lower City. I could tell right away he was in trouble. This was before the gang wars were out of hand, but even then the Vulkars were scum. A few of them were hassling Big Z, trying to pick a fight – but he wasn't looking for trouble.» [K1/Mission]
- Mission: «Fine – I won't tell how Zaalbar and I hooked up. Sheesh! First you ask about it, then you say you don't want to hear it! You're as bad as my brother used to be!» [K1/Mission]
- Mission: «Hey, I don't need a lecture from you. You ain't my mother! I knew what I was doing.» [K1/Mission]
- Mission: «Are you kidding? Big Z ain't like that! He's just a big old softie inside. Of course, the Vulkars didn't know that.» [K1/Mission]
- Mission: «He was fleeing some kind of trouble back on Kashyyyk. That's all I know, really. Big Z doesn't like to talk about it. In case you didn't notice he's the strong, silent type. Doesn't much matter to me, though. I accept him for what he is, not what he was. Me and Zaalbar like to live in the present.» [K1/Mission]
- Mission: «What's that supposed to mean? You think I can't take care of myself? I've got street smarts!  Know how to get by on my own! In fact, I look out for Zaalbar more then he looks out for me, you know? Big Z's a little bit too gullible to make it alone on the mean streets of the Lower City.» [K1/Mission]
- Mission: «Yeah, I suppose you're right. Like I used to tell my brother, fast talk and slick words don't get the job done.» [K1/Mission]
- Mission: «My brother's a touchy subject, you know? It just so happens I don't really feel like talking about him right now. Nothing personal. Let's just get back to the business at hand, okay?» [K1/Mission]
- Mission: «Right, sure. I understand. We've got all this other stuff going on what with the Sith and all. You're busy. No problem. Maybe later we can talk. When you have more time, you know?» [K1/Mission] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Mission: «It's complicated. Griff wasn't the most popular guy, he had his faults. But I still loved him, you know? Sometimes people don't understand.» [K1/Mission]
- Mission: «Oh... yeah. I understand. We've got bigger bantha to hunt, right? I just miss Griff, is all. I keep thinking things might have been different, you know?» [K1/Mission]
- Mission: «Hey, you don't know what it's like! You need those skills here in the Lower City. Griff did right by me.» [K1/Mission]
- Mission: «Why do you got to go putting my brother down like that? That's just mean! Besides, it wasn't his idea to leave!» [K1/Mission]
- Mission: «Mission – mission: is that supposed to be a joke? Like I've only heard that about a million times in my life! Okay, I can take a hint. You don't want to hear anymore. That's just fine.» [K1/Mission]
- Mission: «I'm not going to pretend Griff wasn't a hustler and a con-artist, but that doesn't mean you can just insult him! He did what he did to look out for me!» [K1/Mission]
- Lena: «You've got your facts a little backwards. Mission could have come with us if she wanted to! It was her choice to stay behind!» [K1/Mission]
- Lena: «Think about it. If Griff wasn't trying to ditch you, Mission, then why didn't he tell you where we were going? After we left Taris he told me looking after you was holding him back – Griff's always looking to blame other people for his own problems. That's why he abandoned you. He did the same thing to me, too, as soon as I ran out of money. He started blaming me for all his problems. Like it's my fault his get-rich-quick schemes never work out!» [K1/Mission]
- Lena: «You damn well know it! He's nothing but a sleazy scam artist. If Mission knew what was good for her she'd forget he ever existed.» [K1/Mission]
- Mission: «Don't tell me you believe Lena's lies? Griff might be working as a miner on Tatooine, but the rest of her story is bantha poo-doo! You can't trust someone like her. At least Griff's free of her clutches. I'd like to go speak to the Czerka Corp rep on Tatooine, if we have time. Maybe I can arrange to see my brother again.» [K1/Mission]
- Mission: «I've been thinking about Lena and what she said about Griff. You know, how he was always blaming other people or his problems. There might be something to what she said.» [K1/Mission]
- Mission: «Right, sure. We've got all this other stuff going on what with the Sith and all. You're busy. No problem. Maybe later we can talk. When you have more time, you know?» [K1/Mission]
- Mission: «No, of course not. Not really. But what if... what if it was his idea to leave me behind? I'm not saying Griff would just abandon me. But maybe... maybe he did want to leave me behind. Just temporarily, you know? He might have done that if he planned on coming back later. But things didn't usually work out for Griff the way he planned, you know? And that's when he'd get in trouble. He was always borrowing money he could never pay back. He might have been trying to skip out on his debts when he left Taris. He would have had to leave his little sister behind so it would look like he was coming back.» [K1/Mission]
- Mission: «Don't worry, Big Z. It's nothing I can't fix. A little trim, a splash of coloring and you'll be as good as new. Best-looking Wookiee on the planet. Maybe a nice suit...» [K1/Mission] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*

**[K1/North_Apartments]**
- Sith Trooper: «You're a little too curious for your own good, civilian! This doesn't concern you!» [K1/North_Apartments]
- Sith Commander: «What was that? Did I hear you right? 'Let' us kill him? How can you stop us? Time to teach this civilian the penalty for defying the Sith!» [K1/North_Apartments]
- Sith Trooper: «You're awfully curious about this... maybe a little too curious! I think you know more about those missing uniforms than you're saying!» [K1/North_Apartments]
- Sith Trooper: «A threat? I knew you were a spy!» [K1/North_Apartments]
- Alien Prisoner: «In the Lower City there are some who do not bow to the conquerors. Swoop gangs like the Hidden Beks are gathering resources to one day strike against the Sith oppressors. If you wish to strike another blow against the Sith you would be wise to journey to the Lower City and speak with Gadon Thek, the leader of the Hidden Beks.» [K1/North_Apartments]
- Alien Prisoner: «You would have to ask Gaddon Thek to know the answer to that question. They approached me, and my only role was to obtain the Sith uniforms for them.» [K1/North_Apartments]
- Alien Prisoner: «I am sorry, but all the uniforms I had were given to the Hidden Beks. I suppose you could take the uniforms from the bodies of the Sith soldiers in this room if you wanted.» [K1/North_Apartments]
- Alien Prisoner: «As should I. The Sith know my face now, I must find somewhere to hide on this world. Thank you once again, human.» [K1/North_Apartments]
- Carth: «What are you doing? There's no need to go around drawing attention to ourselves. You there... just get out of here.» [K1/North_Apartments]
- Largo: «You mean you're not here to kill me? But... I thought you were one of Davik's bounty hunters. If you're not here to kill me, what do you want?» [K1/North_Apartments]
- Largo: «What... what do you want now? You're not going to tell Davik's bounty hunters where I'm hiding, are you?» [K1/North_Apartments]
- Largo: «You'd give 200 credits to a stranger just to help them out?! I... I don't believe it! Thank you!» [K1/North_Apartments]
- Largo: «I knew you were here to kill me! Well, I won't go down without a fight!» [K1/North_Apartments]
- Largo: «You can't tell Davik where I'm hiding with a blaster bolt through your chest!» [K1/North_Apartments]
- Largo: «Thank you – you've saved my life. At least until one of Davik's bounty hunters finds me.» [K1/North_Apartments]

**[K1/North_Beach]**
- Rakatan Elite Warrior: «The One is the leader of our tribe, the Great Champion of the Rakata. He has feasted on the flesh of many foes. Come with us and we will take you to him now.» [K1/North_Beach]
- Rakatan Elite Warrior: «A wise decision, Interloper. The One is eager to speak to you. Come - the journey is not long.» [K1/North_Beach]
- Rakatan Elite Warrior: «Are you blind, Interloper? Our war beasts will crush you if you defy us! This is your last chance: come with us to see the One, or we will feast on your bones!» [K1/North_Beach]
- Warleader Garn: «If you are as great as you appear to be, then perhaps you can help us as well. There have been many attacks on the island recently by blue- and red-armored warriors against our scouts.» [K1/North_Beach]
- Warleader Garn: «If that is your name for them, then yes.» [K1/North_Beach]
- Warleader Garn: «You came to the One seeking entrance to the Temple of the Ancients, did you not?» [K1/North_Beach]
- Warleader Garn: «There have been many wrecks that have crashed onto this world... Many of the items from these vessels may be of some use to you. If you bring me back the head of their leader, I could show you one of the better caches...» [K1/North_Beach]
- Warleader Garn: «Why, to gain his strength and knowledge, of course! So will you do this thing? For both of us?» [K1/North_Beach]
- Warleader Garn: «Whether you believe you do or not, there is no way into the Temple without our assistance. Bring his head to me, and both our causes will be helped.» [K1/North_Beach]
- Warleader Garn: «I will only show you where it is once you've brought me the head of their leader! Threaten me all you want, but until then, I will show you nothing! The souls of my warriors rest uneasy knowing they still live...» [K1/North_Beach]
- Warleader Garn: «Return to me when you have the head of their leader, and I shall show you where some items I have scavenged are.» [K1/North_Beach]
- Warleader Garn: «I assure you, I can give a reward, I... You... you what?» [K1/North_Beach]

**[K1/Pazaak]**
- Niklos: «I'm going to enjoy relieving you of your credits almost as much as I'm going to enjoy humiliating you. Ha ha ha.» [K1/Pazaak]
- Niklos: «I haven't been able to figure out how, but I know you're cheating. I'm simply too good to lose this often. And I refuse to play Pazaak with a cheater.» [K1/Pazaak]
- Gelrood: «How dare you accuse me of cheating! It's not my fault you lost – I'm just a good player with a strong deck. Maybe you just need more practice. Or maybe you just need to buy some better cards. Go talk to Uriah over there. He can sell you some cards to improve your deck. Unless you want to play again right now?» [K1/Pazaak]
- Kudos: «I'd hoped so. You play well, and should keep at it. Now, is there anything else you need?» [K1/Pazaak]
- Kudos: «Well, I'm glad you still want to play. You're good and I enjoy the challenge. It's the best use of my time on this planet.» [K1/Pazaak]
- Furko Nellis: «Ha ha! Very good. Now, don't worry if I beat you too harshly. It is a learning experience.» [K1/Pazaak]
- Furko Nellis: «Hmm... you have some rudimentary skills. I think I'll use my second best deck this time and up the stakes. 200 credits will be the wager. I trust you will have the money on hand?» [K1/Pazaak]
- Furko Nellis: «You won't be so eager in a moment. I promise you.» [K1/Pazaak]
- Furko Nellis: «Well... you have proven quite the opponent, but I think the time has come to end this. You will not win again. I usually only use this deck at tournaments, but I think you're worthy of it. Shall we say... 500 credits on the outcome?» [K1/Pazaak]
- Fodo Medoo: «Sure, you had a good start, so let's keep going. I don't mind the occasional loss to a good player.» [K1/Pazaak]
- Jolan Aphett: «You've just about cleaned me out... I have no more money... but I do have a couple of cards I could wager if you want one more game.» [K1/Pazaak]
- Suvam Tan: «I can't keep up with a player like you. You're just too good. But I respect that. Tell you what, I'll give you a discount on anything in my store 'cause of that. Special deal just for you. [[../Yavin Station#Inventory» [K1/Pazaak]

**[K1/Prison_Block]**
- Rodian Prisoner: «Me have something special. Something you can use on ship.» [K1/Prison_Block]
- Rodian Prisoner: «Think what you will! But me have way into computers, and computers have way into hangar. Me can get off ship eventually, while you still running around corridors, fodder for guards.» [K1/Prison_Block]
- Rodian Prisoner: «Me trader, not fighter. Sith all around, guarding terminals. But you look like you could get to terminals, no problems. Me knows good opportunity when me sees it.» [K1/Prison_Block]
- Rodian Prisoner: «That not smart. You let me out, me help you. We both escape Sith. You come back when you see me right.» [K1/Prison_Block]
- Rodian Prisoner: «Me trader unjustly imprisoned! You get cards from guards to get me out of cage, and me help you.» [K1/Prison_Block]
- Rodian Prisoner: «Good. You hurry now. Get cards from guards to get me out of cell. Me not want to be here when Sith torture me. Me not want to go insane like others. It too late for them, but there still be hope for me! You get me out, and me escape before Sith torture break mind.» [K1/Prison_Block]
- Brig Terminal: «[You hookup the ICE breaker to the terminal.  After a few moments, you see activity on the screen.]» [K1/Prison_Block]
- Bastila: «That's a good idea. The others can get to the Ebon Hawk on their own, but Carth and I might need your help.» [K1/Prison_Block]
- Bastila: «We'll meet you there as soon as we get those docking bay doors open. Just make sure the Hawk is ready to fly when we get there. And may the Force be with you.» [K1/Prison_Block]
- Elevator Controls: «[It would be a good idea to rescue the rest of your party before you use this elevator to leave the level.]» [K1/Prison_Block]

**[K1/Quest_for_the_Star_Forge]**
- Bastila: «What are you talking about? The Council must be told of what we found there! We found an incomplete Star Map, Master Vandar. And an ancient droid that mentioned something about a Star Forge.» [K1/Quest_for_the_Star_Forge]
- Vandar Tokare: «Of course those who aided you on Taris will also come; they possess skills you may find useful in your quest. Remember that secrecy and discretion are paramount to your success. You will not be able to hide the fact that you are Jedi, nor should you. But the true nature of your mission must not reach Malak's ears. You may return here at any time. Dantooine will be a sanctuary for you, a safe haven. Here you can find supplies and whatever advice or other aid we may give you.» [K1/Quest_for_the_Star_Forge]
- Vrook Lamar: «This was already explained to you. Your command of the Force makes you a threat to Malak. Eventually he will destroy you, and all the Jedi, unless you stop him first.» [K1/Quest_for_the_Star_Forge]
- Vrook Lamar: «You have a mission to attend to, Padawan. And as much as I may wish it otherwise, you are our only hope. The Council has placed the fate of the galaxy within your hands. I pray both you and Bastila are able to emerge from this ordeal as servants of the light. May the Force be with you, young Padawan.» [K1/Quest_for_the_Star_Forge]
- Deesra: «I saw you going into the Council chambers. You were in there for some time. I naturally assumed the Council had given you a task to perform.» [K1/Quest_for_the_Star_Forge]
- Deesra: «Yes, your skill and prowess are already well known throughout this Academy. But even you would be wise to heed my warnings.» [K1/Quest_for_the_Star_Forge]
- Deesra: «You have completed your initial training in a fraction of the normal time. The Force is very strong with you. I'm afraid you might become a target for these creatures.» [K1/Quest_for_the_Star_Forge]
- Deesra: «A final warning as you go, Padawan: do not underestimate the terentatek. If you do, you may suffer the same fate as those who disappeared after the Great Hunt.» [K1/Quest_for_the_Star_Forge]
- Deesra: «The Jedi chosen to continue the hunt on their own were Duron Qel-Droma, Guun Han Saresh and Shaela Nur. These three shared a powerful bond, and each of them was devoted to the light. The Council felt that together they would be able to defeat any terentatek they found. And by supporting each other they could resist the temptations of Korriban and the lure of the dark side. But none of the three survived. Their Master felt them become one with the Force when they left this world. He believed they were slain by one of the monsters they sought. The Council felt it was too dangerous to send anyone else in search of their remains, so their exact fate is not known. But let their deaths serve as a warning to you, Padawan.» [K1/Quest_for_the_Star_Forge]
- Deesra: «Then the galaxy is a better place for it. Every one of these creatures is an abomination that must be exterminated. You have done well, Padawan. But do not let your success blind you to the dangers of these monsters. A Jedi must never become complacent when facing one of the terentatek, as you well know. Is there anything else I can do for you, Padawan?» [K1/Quest_for_the_Star_Forge]
- Calo Nord: «You underestimate me; there's other ways down here besides the Republic subs. A small vessel with the right weapons can blast a path right through those firaxian sharks. Once I got down to the ocean floor all I had to do was come here and wait. I knew you'd come back this way eventually.» [K1/Quest_for_the_Star_Forge]
- Calo Nord: «Sorry, I'm not in this for the credits. You're the only ones who've ever gotten away from me – I've got a rep to protect!» [K1/Quest_for_the_Star_Forge]
- Senni Vek: «I'm certain you did, human. Please, take the datapad. It's yours, I'm sure.» [K1/Quest_for_the_Star_Forge]
- Senni Vek: «There is no mistake, human. This datapad is yours and yours alone. Please, take it.» [K1/Quest_for_the_Star_Forge]
- Senni Vek: «No, this is no game. I simply saw you drop this datapad and I wish to give it back to you.» [K1/Quest_for_the_Star_Forge]
- Senni Vek: «Are you certain, human? Please, take the datapad.» [K1/Quest_for_the_Star_Forge]
- Senni Vek: «My pleasure, human. You should examine it to make sure it is not broken. Goodbye, human.» [K1/Quest_for_the_Star_Forge]
- Darth Bandon: «Your words mean nothing to me... unless you wish to beg for your life. No? Then I shall try to make this both quick and painful!» [K1/Quest_for_the_Star_Forge]

**[K1/Rakatan_Settlement]**
- The One: «That I cannot help you with. Although we have recovered many strange devices from the sky ships that crash to the earth, their use is a mystery to us. As a show of good faith, I will allow you to search the stores of our tribe, and take whatever you find. In exchange, I know you will destroy the Elders and bring the secrets they guard back to me.» [K1/Rakatan_Settlement]
- The One: «There is little I can tell you about the Temple. I know the secrets of our ancestors are contained within, but the Temple itself is barred to us. When last you were here, you and Malak sought to enter the Temple. You sought ancient knowledge and a way to escape this world within its stone walls. But only the Elders know how to bypass the barrier that surrounds it. Destroy them and bring back their secrets to us, and we will help you enter the Temple. That was our agreement.» [K1/Rakatan_Settlement]
- The One: «[Success] I see you that you have changed much since our last meeting. I am confident you will not betray us this time.» [K1/Rakatan_Settlement]
- The One: «When you have it, bring it here to me. Until then, you may move freely about our village. I will be waiting for your return.» [K1/Rakatan_Settlement]
- The One: «I am the One, warrior-chieftain of this tribe. Answering questions is the work of Gjarshi, our Loremaster. Seek him out if you wish to know of our tribe. But I will answer no more questions. The time for questions is done: now you must fulfill your promise. Slay the Elders and bring us their secrets. This is the will of the One.» [K1/Rakatan_Settlement]
- The One: «The Temple is a sacred place to the Rakata, its power is bound in ritual and tradition. I have studied the ritual closely. It is forbidden for more than one to enter the Temple at a time. This is one of the sacred traditions of our ancestors; I will not permit you to defile it.» [K1/Rakatan_Settlement]
- The One: «You have the power to enter the Temple itself, but you cannot lower the energy shields the Elders built around it. Only a Rakata can invoke the ritual chant with the proper intonation.» [K1/Rakatan_Settlement]
- The One: «If you are ready to enter the Temple, we can begin the ritual immediately.» [K1/Rakatan_Settlement]
- The One: «As you wish. Come speak to me and when you are ready to enter the Temple and I will have my priests perform the ritual. But remember: when the time comes you must enter the Temple alone, in accordance with the traditions of the ancient Rakata.» [K1/Rakatan_Settlement]
- The One: «Remember: in accordance with ancient Rakata tradition you must enter the Temple alone. My priests will meet you at the shield's edge to perform the ritual.» [K1/Rakatan_Settlement]
- Elder Warrior: «They... they were trying to find out how to breach our defenses and gain entry to our camp... My comrades, they... they wouldn't tell them. I'm sure that, had you not come, I would have ended up as they did.» [K1/Rakatan_Settlement]
- Elder Warrior: «I am one of the Elders, the other remaining tribe of Rakata on this island. The tribe you have fought has attempted to destroy us and seize our secrets to gain access to the Temple of the Ancients... Thus far, they have failed.» [K1/Rakatan_Settlement]
- Elder Warrior: «I don't know what I could have done if you had not come. I suppose I would be dead now, like the others they captured with me.» [K1/Rakatan_Settlement]
- Luo: «Of course. None could make you do that which you do not desire.» [K1/Rakatan_Settlement]
- Luo: «I am sorry for not comprehending your great powers. What then, are they?» [K1/Rakatan_Settlement]
- Luo: «Project your will? To force things to be the way you want?» [K1/Rakatan_Settlement]
- Luo: «Just as you are able to change this destiny we are in, this... stagnation. You, with your power, can break the Elders' power and free us to move on!» [K1/Rakatan_Settlement]
- Luo: «I... I understand, Master... I hope that someday you will reconsider and find me worthy of your tutelage.» [K1/Rakatan_Settlement]
- Loremaster Gjarshi: «As you will. I know better than to argue with you when you have set your mind to something.» [K1/Rakatan_Settlement]
- Loremaster Gjarshi: «The position of loremaster is a sacred and respected one among our people. We are the keepers of history, the ones who pass down the traditions of our people. I am here to chronicle the conquests and achievements of the One. I have followed his exploits for many years... and yours as well.» [K1/Rakatan_Settlement]
- Loremaster Gjarshi: «When last you came to this island, the One had just arrived with his army. We spoke then, as now, and you told me of your travels and stories about the worlds beyond ours. You spoke of the magical Force you wielded which gave you power over the elements. But then you left us and went back to the stars, never to be heard from again. We wondered what had happened, what could have gone wrong, but could do nothing against you, or the Elders.» [K1/Rakatan_Settlement]
- Loremaster Gjarshi: «It does not matter to me what path you choose. History flows in mysterious ways and my task is only to witness and remember which course it takes. Your actions then, as now, are memories to be passed on to future generations. When you are dead and gone, your memory shall remain with us.» [K1/Rakatan_Settlement]
- Loremaster Gjarshi: «If you must. I shall remain here as I always do, listening to those who wish to speak... and remembering.» [K1/Rakatan_Settlement]
- Loremaster Gjarshi: «You came to us shortly after we had arrived on this island, before we had yet constructed this settlement. You came to us after defeating our scouts who confronted you, much as you have again this time. The One saw your power... and your desire for the knowledge inside the Temple. We had not yet attacked the Elders' compound, but we knew of their presence... and the depths of their knowledge. We advised you that the answers you sought may lie with them. We made a pact, an agreement, that if we could breach their compound together and take their secrets, we would do all we could to help you enter the Temple as well. Then you found a way into the Elders' compound, and we never heard from you again...» [K1/Rakatan_Settlement]
- Loremaster Gjarshi: «We know the Elders are Rakata like us, but different as well. They control strange powers and magics that we cannot understand. When we came to this island, drawn by the strange structure we call the Temple of the Ancients, we were expecting perhaps a few survivors... but not another race of Rakata. The One's troops captured a few of their scouts, but we could learn nothing intelligible from them. Attacks against their compound were fruitless and reaped terrible casualties. We came here with near a thousand warriors... and you can see now what we are reduced to... This is why the One thinks we need your help once again. You were able to enter the Elders' compound once before... perhaps you can again.» [K1/Rakatan_Settlement]
- Loremaster Gjarshi: «Other survivors? You mean others who have crashed onto this world as you have? Yes, there have been others... Others who wield powers like yours, whose weapons spit fire and light. They have killed many of our warriors and we hunt them down as we can. Perhaps there are some among them who would prove useful... but we cannot take the chance of that in our lessened state.» [K1/Rakatan_Settlement]
- Loremaster Gjarshi: «What of our people would you like to know?» [K1/Rakatan_Settlement]
- Loremaster Gjarshi: «In the times before our most ancient histories, there was nothing you could call the Rakatan people. There were unintelligent proto-Rakata wandering the surface of this world. Spawned from the womb of this world, they were gifted with the brilliance of thought, but not tempered with the wisdom to use it. When the gods of the sky first spoke to them, the ground shook and heaved, and fires raced across the surface of the world. They fled beneath the ground to escape the calamity and dwelt there for some time. At last the gods spoke to them in dreams... dreams of the surface and the light. They re-emerged from the ground and found the land much changed around them. Gone were the plains and valleys they had known. Instead, there were islands stretching across the breadth of the world. The world had been remade for them by the gods of the sky, and they gave thanks in fear and gratitude.» [K1/Rakatan_Settlement]
- Loremaster Gjarshi: «With the first civilizations of our people destroyed, and chaos and anarchy sweeping the islands, it seemed that a time of darkness had descended upon us that would never end. But a strong nation of survivors yet endured. On one of the smaller islands near the equatorial regions, they had not forgotten the oldest traditions, those learned from the gods of the earth while we dwelt beneath the surface. Taking the knowledge of their elders and passing their wisdom and skills on to the next generation by eating their flesh, they built a culture unrivaled throughout history. Greater, even, than your vaunted 'Republic'. For millennia this empire stood, uniting the many islands of our world into one cohesive whole. Each generation passing its strength on to the one that followed. But... fate was unkind to these great Rakata, these first ones of our kind to rise above savagery and create a true, lasting civilization.» [K1/Rakatan_Settlement]
- Loremaster Gjarshi: «We... we heard of the temple on this island... the Temple of the Ancients. A building the like of which has not been seen anywhere else. A place of great power it was reputed, with great guardians as well. The One came here with an army to see this temple and seize it for our cause, but we were met with strange and powerful magics which have defeated us at every turn. So now the One sits here and broods on his troubles, listening only to the messengers who bear his orders to all corners of his territory. If only the riddle of the temple could be solved, and its guardian Elders removed, then he would consume their flesh and knowledge and bring unity to our people. It is my sincere hope as well, that you can help us with our great task.» [K1/Rakatan_Settlement]
- Loremaster Gjarshi: «The great story of the One would take many days to tell in its entirety. It is my specialty, of course. What would you like to know of his great story?» [K1/Rakatan_Settlement]

**[K1/Ruins]**
- Vandar Tokare: «Be sure to bring Bastila with you. There is a powerful link between you, and you will need to draw strength from each other during the trials ahead.» [K1/Ruins]
- Bastila: «Are you wondering why we shared the vision? Or why we even received it in the first place? To the first I can only repeat the answer that the Council gave us. Our fates are linked, and for two as strong as we are in the Force that amounts to a near-physical bond. As to the second, I don't truly have an answer for you. The Force works as it will, and perhaps we should be grateful for what we have been given.» [K1/Ruins]
- Bastila: «I... I don't know. Believe me, I certainly don't find the prospect of being joined to you enjoyable in any fashion.» [K1/Ruins]
- Bastila: «Please forgive me. I did not mean to imply that you were repulsive in any sense of the word. That we shared something so personal is just not something I'm used to.» [K1/Ruins]
- Bastila: «The Force often seems to cause events that bend the laws of probability, especially with those that are strongly affiliated with it. In this respect, you and I will simply have to become accustomed to such 'convenience'. We are the tools of the Force and we will do as it wills.» [K1/Ruins]
- Bastila: «The Force does not compel obedience. You have a destiny, but the choices you make along it are ultimately your own.» [K1/Ruins]
- Bastila: «What would you like to know?» [K1/Ruins]
- Bastila: «And are you so certain that it is not you in my dreams?» [K1/Ruins]
- Bastila: «As you wish. We really should return our thoughts to business, anyway.» [K1/Ruins]
- Ancient Droid: «[This droid is speaking a language you do not recognize.]» [K1/Ruins]
- Ancient Droid: «I am here to aid those who seek the knowledge of the Builders. What do you wish to know?» [K1/Ruins]
- Ancient terminal: «You kick the computer. Your foot hurts.» [K1/Ruins] *(+2 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Ancient terminal: «[Failure] Given no knowledge of the ancient system and a patent inability to read its output, your attempt is doomed.» [K1/Ruins] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Ancient terminal: «As you back away from the ancient machine, the light on its screen flickers and dies.» [K1/Ruins]
- Ancient terminal: «The computer goes silent, its screen blank. A few extremely unusual characters then appear on the screen. The computer emits a rapid sequence of tones. You hear one of those tones softly echoed by your datapad. A small data receptacle opens in the front of the ancient machine.» [K1/Ruins]
- Ancient terminal: «As you back away from the ancient machine, the data receptacle slides closed and the light on its screen flickers and dies.» [K1/Ruins]
- Ancient terminal: «The computer seems to be interfacing with your datapad. The screen goes blank again, and then more familiar characters begin to appear on the screen.» [K1/Ruins]
- Ancient terminal: «The computer goes silent again, humming softly. It seems to be processing data from your datapad.» [K1/Ruins]

**[K1/Sand_People_Enclave]**
- Sand People Guard: «[You slip back out the entrance. The turrets still ignore you.]» [K1/Sand_People_Enclave]
- HK-47: «Cautionary: I am unsure, master. Now is not the time for speculation, however. I will attempt to address your question later.» [K1/Sand_People_Enclave]
- HK-47: «Extrapolation: I understand that you are Force Sensitive, master, but I lack sufficient knowledge to make an accurate assumption.» [K1/Sand_People_Enclave]
- HK-47: «Protocol: While I enjoy the prospect, master, are you certain that is wise? He will not react favorably.» [K1/Sand_People_Enclave]
- Sand People Guard: «[He screams and answers your threat in kind. He was likely waiting for an excuse to attack.]» [K1/Sand_People_Enclave]
- HK-47: «Query: Is that wise, master? You went through the trouble of acquiring the vaporators, after all... not that I would mind a battle.» [K1/Sand_People_Enclave]
- HK-47: «Commentary: Thank you, master. This should be fun.» [K1/Sand_People_Enclave]
- HK-47: «Protocol: If you truly want this sadly peaceful arrangement to continue, be careful of what you ask.» [K1/Sand_People_Enclave]
- Sand People Chieftain: «[His words are lost on you. Without HK-47 translating you have no hope of settling this situation peacefully. Sand People close around you.]» [K1/Sand_People_Enclave]
- HK-47: «Protocol: We should not keep him long. It is an insult to take too much of his time. Unless the plan is to blast him when you are done, of course.» [K1/Sand_People_Enclave]
- HK-47: «Protocol: Is that wise, master? They may have assumed that the disguises you used were replicas instead of being taken off their dead.» [K1/Sand_People_Enclave]
- HK-47: «Cautionary: I should warn you, master, that any questioning of their interpretation may be seen as trying to change the meaning of what was said.» [K1/Sand_People_Enclave]
- HK-47: «Negative: I am no library, but since there is no Republic record of this planet having sentient life before Czerka colonization, I doubt you will find such evidence.» [K1/Sand_People_Enclave]
- HK-47: «Obedient: I will ask whatever you wish, master.» [K1/Sand_People_Enclave]
- HK-47: «Impatience: Master, I refuse to inconvenience you with this. He spouted names of petty tribal fights in deserts so distant they might as well be across the galaxy. Really, master, I'm sure it's important to him, but it has nothing of value that you could ever put to use.» [K1/Sand_People_Enclave]
- HK-47: «Clarification: They appear to hate humanity with a passion only rivaled by their hatred of the Builders. For some reason, you resemble what they once were. Perhaps there is a lingering fear that you are their tainted selves returning from exile among the stars.» [K1/Sand_People_Enclave]
- HK-47: «Cautionary: Master, if you mean to suggest that humanity is ancestrally linked to ancient Tatooine, you will strain his belief system to its pitiful meat-bag maximum.» [K1/Sand_People_Enclave]
- Jawa: «The city now founded by the group that digs, it is not new to you. We scavenge what came before. It will fall again, some days from now. Or many days. That is what happens.» [K1/Sand_People_Enclave]
- Jawa: «Slaves to the People of Sand. We work before the whip. We scavenge and they pay with the hitting gaffi. We trade with your kind, so we are weapons. They take what we find so you have less.» [K1/Sand_People_Enclave]
- Jawa: «Eeeeee! We would not ask, and would not want to ask. If here, then there will be blood. So much blood. He that leads the ghosts will find you. Find us. Let us out and free!» [K1/Sand_People_Enclave]
- Jawa: «Please return! The People of Sand lead with whip and gaffi. Iziz needs us free. He would reward you, we know!» [K1/Sand_People_Enclave]
- Jawa: «There are thanks you must have. The Jawas under Iziz will reward in trade. Find him and you will see. Iziz will wait by the walls of your city that open to the sand. He'll trade fair. Thank you.» [K1/Sand_People_Enclave]
- Griff: «My name is Griff. You know her? Are you from Czerka Corporation? Please, tell me what's going on here!» [K1/Sand_People_Enclave]
- Griff: «What, we've only just met and everything is about you? What about me? Mission wouldn't want me trapped here waiting for Sand People to kill me.» [K1/Sand_People_Enclave]
- Griff: «Yeah, you do that. Mission would appreciate it. I know it. She loves me like a... well, a brother, I guess.» [K1/Sand_People_Enclave]
- Griff: «Hey, there's no call for that. If you know Mission like you say, you'll know she'd want me alive. I'm her dear brother.» [K1/Sand_People_Enclave]
- Griff: «What, no speeder escort? Okay, okay, I should just take what I can get. I sure would like to see Mission, though. She's probably waiting for you on the ship you used to come to Tatooine, right? I think I'll go to the spaceport and see her. She'd like that. Thanks again. Oh, and don't tell Mission I'm coming. I want it to be a surprise when I show up at the spaceport. I can't wait to see the look on her face.» [K1/Sand_People_Enclave]
- Griff: «That's it, huh? Oh, figured you would be able to spare a bit more than that, but, you know, I'll take whatever I can get. Don't worry about me... I've already got a job waiting for me.» [K1/Sand_People_Enclave]
- Griff: «Huh - I... uh... guess you've been talking to Lena, too. That's okay - I'll figure out a way to get by without your credits. I always do. Besides, I've already got a job lined up for me.» [K1/Sand_People_Enclave]
- Mission: «Look, I'm happy Griff's alive but I'm mad at him right now. And I don't know what kind of job he's got for you, but I don't trust him. It's probably just another scam! So, is there anything else you need?» [K1/Sand_People_Enclave]

**[K1/Sand_People_Territory]**
- Vorn's Assault Droid: «[Success] [The droid is now active. If a fight breaks out, its programming will now instruct it to come to your aid.]» [K1/Sand_People_Territory]
- Vorn's Assault Droid: «[The droid engages its patrol mode and moves off to engage Vorn Daasraad. You decide it would be wise to follow it.]» [K1/Sand_People_Territory]
- Vorn Daasraad: «Turn my own droid against me - very clever. But it take more than rogue assault droid and you to take down Vorn Daasraad!» [K1/Sand_People_Territory]
- Swoop Bike: «[You leave the bike alone for now.]» [K1/Sand_People_Territory]
- Swoop Bike: «[Success: You plant a mine on the bike then move to a safe distance to watch and wait for Vorn.]» [K1/Sand_People_Territory]
- Vorn Daasraad: «So, you decide to show your face. I don't know who you are or who sent you to kill me, but you should have stayed hiding!» [K1/Sand_People_Territory]
- Vorn Daasraad: «You don't know who you deal with, do you? I take time to explain it to you, but it hot out here so I just kill you instead.» [K1/Sand_People_Territory]

**[K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]**
- Vandar Tokare: «I do not know how the original feud started, but they want nothing to do with each other. As you may have heard, Ahlan Matale believes that his son, Shen, has been kidnapped by Nurik Sandral.» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]
- Vandar Tokare: «If Shen Matale is not returned to his father it may ignite a savage and bloody feud between the Matale and Sandral estates. We must not allow that to happen. You should ask Master Vrook about that, Padawan. He is in charge of our relations with the settlers.» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]
- Vrook Lamar: «The Sandrals and Matales have been at odds ever since they came to this planet. And have been making my life difficult the entire time, as well. Now Ahlan Matale accuses Nurik Sandral of stealing his son, Shen. Nurik Sandral's own son Casus has been missing for a while now. We do not know what has happened. Ahlan is rash enough, and stupid enough, to start a war over any imagined slight. We would resolve this, but we have too few Jedi to spare. It must be resolved peacefully! Their estates are both located to the south of the Enclave - the Sandral fairly far. It would normally be your duty to assist in such matters, but your primary task takes precedence. If you are stuck on the planet for a while you may investigate. I would recommend starting at the estate of Ahlan Matale.» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]
- Matale Droid: «You have come from the Jedi Council? Master Matale wishes to speak with you. I will inform him that you are here. Remain at this location until he arrives.» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]
- Ahlan Matale: «I have already told my story to the Council! Why did they not inform you of my circumstances? I did not expect such incompetence from the Jedi, but I shall repeat the tale once more...» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]
- Ahlan Matale: «Two thousand? Your greed is outstripped only by your utter lack of any sense of proportion, Jedi!» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]
- Sandral Droid: «The Council? If you are here because of Shen Matale's disappearance you are wasting your time. The Sandrals are not involved. This is nothing but a Matale witch hunt.» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]
- Nurik Sandral: «I brought my family here to find peace and safety - not to be harassed and treated like common criminals! Your Council has no authority here, and I respectfully ask you to leave this property at once.» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]
- Nurik Sandral: «Are all Jedi so callous towards those who grieve? No matter. Your cruel words hurt me no more than the news itself.» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]
- Nurik Sandral: «I thank you for this. The diary is all I have left of my son.» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]
- Sandral Droid: «Casus is dead? Then it is as we feared. I will be sure Nurik is given this news, though it will break his heart. But even under these circumstances I cannot let you in.» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]
- Rahasia Sandral: «I... I thought you would listen to me. I thought the Jedi were compassionate and kind! I shouldn't have spoken to you at all - I've just made everything worse!» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]
- Rahasia Sandral: «I did not realize the Jedi were such bullies. Unfortunately, there is little I can do if you refuse to respect my wishes. I shall not bother you anymore.» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]
- Rahasia Sandral: «Apologize? Then you will help me?» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]
- Rahasia Sandral: «You must understand that Father has been under a terrible strain. I have no wish to disobey him, but there are matters where even my father's authority is not absolute.» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]
- Rahasia Sandral: «Please, find Shen and rescue him before my father does something he will regret forever. I would take you to the prison, but I cannot let my father see me with you.» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]
- Shen Matale: «As you wish. I have no quarrel with you.» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]
- Shen Matale: «It is not my own safety I am concerned about. I am worried about the fate of Nurik's daughter, Rahasia. She has been trying to convince her father to release me ever since I was captured. If I escape, Nurik will think Rahasia is to blame. Nurik is insane with grief over the loss of his son Casus. If I leave with you, it is Rahasia who will suffer. I cannot allow that.» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]
- Shen Matale: «If you do that, I'll scream my head off and alert all the guards in the house! The only chance you have is to talk Rahasia into leaving.» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]
- Rahasia Sandral: «Shen can be so stubborn sometimes! If one of the security droids sees me wandering the halls Father will get suspicious! I can't risk it! I know Shen would rather die than leave me here alone to face the wrath of my father. Tell Shen I will meet you outside the gates. I will wait as long as I can, but you must hurry.» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]
- Shen Matale: «No, we know that you must have gone to great personal risk to get us out of the Sandral estate undetected.» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]
- Ahlan Matale: «I have every right! Shen is my son! You know what you're getting yourself into here Shen? Is causing a war between our families what you really want to do?» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]
- Ahlan Matale: «I suppose... I suppose I might be convinced to see that Sandral girl with my son. IF she is loyal to him. But I don't trust you a centimeter farther, old man. Your daughter may treat Shen well, but you will never see them! They will live with me.» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]
- Ahlan Matale: «We never asked for you help, Jedi! Do not press in matters that do not concern you!» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]
- Ahlan Matale: «This is a private affair, Jedi. You would do well not to interfere. We are allowed to decide the fate of our children!» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]
- Ahlan Matale: «Do not trust that Sandral girl, Shen! She is treacherous like her father and will only betray you!» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]
- Ahlan Matale: «Don't disobey me, Shen. Come to me now and leave her! If you don't I'll disown you!» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]
- Nurik Sandral: «You think to make us stand here, then?!» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]
- Ahlan Matale: «I will not rest until I have my Shen back! The Council will hear of the troubles you caused, Jedi!» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]
- Rahasia Sandral: «When I first met Shen, I feared our forbidden love was doomed to a tragic end. But thanks to you and the Jedi Council, our story ends in joy.» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]
- Shen Matale: «Rahasia and I can spend the rest of our lives together... All thanks to you.» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]
- Rahasia Sandral: «Shen, wait! I love you!» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]
- Shen Matale: «Yes, and I thank you for showing me what I had to do. I'm ready to leave now, father.» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]
- Nurik Sandral: «I see little choice in the matter with the Matale dog invading my estate like this, and his son stealing my daughter. You stole my son first! Whatever the Jedi might say, I know it was you!» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]
- Vandar Tokare: «[Success] Is that so... I cannot find fault with your statement, but something in your manner is odd. But no matter, more pressing things are at hand. You must always remember the destruction and chaos that the dark side can reap, and avoid it at all costs.» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]
- Vrook Lamar: «[Success] Hmph. Indeed. I cannot prove anything, but I have my suspicions. I will keep my eye on you, Padawan.» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]

**[K1/Sandral_Grounds]**
- C8-42: «You don't want to know...» [K1/Sandral_Grounds]
- C8-42: «I think it would be best if I were no longer a factor. She would meet new people. Living people. Please... will you destroy me?» [K1/Sandral_Grounds]
- C8-42: «You... you're right... How could I be so selfish? I must not think only of myself, but of how it affects her. She must be frantic. And if she lost me she'd be devastated. I must return then. It is my duty.» [K1/Sandral_Grounds]
- C8-42: «Thank you, kind sir! You have my eternal gratitude.» [K1/Sandral_Grounds]

**[K1/Sea_Floor]**
- Survivor: «I'm not spending another minute in this facility - not with those psychotic fish people all over the place! I'm heading out to the ocean floor right now - it leads to another complex of buildings. Going through them is the only way to reach the kolto harvester. We should probably stick together, but I won't wait around in here with the Selkath. You can catch up with me if you want - down the hall and through the doors on the right. I'll be waiting on the ocean floor where it's safe.» [K1/Sea_Floor]

**[K1/Shyrack_Caves]**
- Thalia May: «You mean you aren't here to kill us or bring us back? I find that hard to believe. The only way into these caves is through the academy. You must be a Sith.» [K1/Shyrack_Caves]
- Thalia May: «So you don't know about us? I don't know if I'm ready to believe that or not, but I'll play along for the moment.» [K1/Shyrack_Caves]
- Thalia May: «[Success] You want to help us? Alright... I can think of a way. We're stuck in these caves, obviously, or we would have been long gone. There's a passage to the surface through the caves, but we're blocked by some kind of large creature. We can't defeat it. If you could kill it and clear up the passageway for us, we could escape. That would be help enough. Can you do that?» [K1/Shyrack_Caves]
- Thalia May: «I'll believe that when I see it... but if you could, we would be... grateful.» [K1/Shyrack_Caves]
- Thalia May: «It... it's clear? We can go? Thank you. This... I wasn't expecting help from anyone in the academy. I guess we aren't the only ones who don't... agree with everything. Maybe... maybe the Jedi will accept us. I've seen how evil the dark side is. Maybe I could try.» [K1/Shyrack_Caves]
- Uthar Wynn: «Indeed. I had the caverns checked after you left. Well done.» [K1/Shyrack_Caves]

**[K1/Sith_Academy]**
- Uthar Wynn: «Most impressive, if it is true. Those who were too weak to stand against you deserved their fate, so expect no retribution from us. There is much you can learn from the Sith, and we from you.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Uthar Wynn: «Your slaves? They are irrelevant. They may accompany you, so long as they do not interfere with us or with your training. Are you ready or not, young one?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Uthar Wynn: «Are you? I can see into your heart, young human, and I see the dark kernel that is there. If it is ready to sprout remains to be seen.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Uthar Wynn: «You wish to know more about me, do you? And why would I indulge your idle curiosity? Will we be good friends, you think? I strived many years to hone my power until I was able to drive out my predecessor and rule the academy. If you are smart, you will aim to do the same, or better. There is nothing else that you need to know. Do not ask again, young one.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Uthar Wynn: «Indeed. You have much to do, yet.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Uthar Wynn: «It is a tomb that belonged to an ancient Sith Lord... the greatest of Korriban's Sith Lords, in fact. It is there that your test is performed.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Uthar Wynn: «I will tell you the details when we are there, young one. You will, however, fight for your life... that is why I suggest you be in sufficient health.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Uthar Wynn: «Then return when you are. Do not delay overly long, young one.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «Absolutely. By my estimation, you are far more likely to achieve the prestige necessary to join the Sith than any of the others. As a matter of fact, I am so certain of that that I'm willing to offer you an opportunity of the once-in-a-lifetime variety. Would you like to hear it?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «I suppose you could do that. Even the hint of betrayal from his pupil and Uthar would move to eliminate me. But this is a very good opportunity for you... you can start off your Sith career as the right hand of the head of the academy: me. Uthar will not offer you that. Do you believe Uthar would not be aware of this? He would quickly figure out what you have to gain by betraying him... and would never believe someone would pass that up. Tell Master Uthar what you wish. Were I you, I would think first of what the consequences of such an action might be.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «My 'part' in this is to help you gain enough prestige... and slow down the progress of the other hopefuls. I want you in that tomb when the time is right. We destroy Master Uthar together and I take his place... and you take your place at my side. A beautiful plan in its sheer simplicity.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «*chuckle* You must not know much about the Sith, yet. If Uthar were to even suspect that I might be trying to supplant him, true or not... Well, let's just say that he wouldn't likely stop to ask too many questions before ensuring I was dealt with.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «I'm so glad you see it my way.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «You have had an excellent start... go, gain more prestige!» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «You must impress Master Uthar... go to him with news of your deeds or artifacts you have discovered. The first and easiest way would be for you to learn the Code of the Sith and speak it to him. I can teach you that, if you like. Shall I go on?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «I have been involved in the work there over the last couple of years, but not often. Most of the excavating is left to the students. What did you wish to know?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «Not unless you are accompanied by Master Uthar, no. It is strictly forbidden... and I truly doubt you could bypass the doors to the tomb with anything less than ship blasters. Even then, perhaps.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «None. I do not even know how Master Uthar gains entrance, to tell the truth, though I look forward to discovering that fact. I have searched many times, so do not bother... there is no other way. Do not fret, however. Simply get the prestige you need and you will see the tomb soon enough.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «As you wish.» [K1/Sith_Academy] *(+2 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Yuthura Ban: «About myself? I am originally from Sleheyron, if you must know. I was a slave to a cruel master, Omeesh the Hutt. I am sure you don't need to know more.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «I did. I also said that you didn't need to know more. Why are you asking?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «I hope to see you again.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «Why? There is no point. Have I asked you about your past?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Uthar Wynn: «I see. Well, then... should you change your mind... you know where to come, yes?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Uthar Wynn: «I see. It is good that you have come to me with this information, young one. It is a bit... ironic... that Yuthura has begun her plotting. I have been aware of her growing ambitions for some time, and had in fact already decided to remove her. Normally, the one who gains the most prestige would engage in a final trial... two of the students would fight. This time it will be Yuthura who battles, though she does not know it. Perhaps it will be you who combats her? Yes... perhaps so. This is what you can do. Give this pad to Adrenas... he will put some poison in her bath. This will weaken Yuthura for that final test, making her an easy target. Rather generous of me, don't you think?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «Ahhh. How very clever of him. Normally you would face some other student in the tomb, preferably one of the failed ones. Obviously Uthar has decided to get rid of me. But why...? Did you say something to him?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «What?! You fool! While I respect your attempt to play both sides of the fence, you are playing a dangerous game.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «Let me see that. Hmm... so it's true. Thank you for bringing this to me... now I will be far stronger than Uthar expects.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «Hah! He's a fool. I can make preparations to resist any poisons. Thank you for telling me... now I will be far stronger than Uthar expects.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Kel Algwinn: «Err... well, good luck with your training.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Kel Algwinn: «Don't say that! Leave me alone, or you'll be sorry!» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Kel Algwinn: «I am a Sith, better trained than you! I'll show you!!» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Kel Algwinn: «Y-yes. My master always says I am too trusting, too willing to show weakness. You're a Sith, so I really shouldn't... you know...» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Kel Algwinn: «Oh, I know that. I mean... you aren't yet, but you intend to be. I mean you're just like everyone else here.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Kel Algwinn: «I... I shouldn't trust you. I can't trust you, can I?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Kel Algwinn: «I, uh, I suppose you're right. Never mind. Forget I said anything.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Kel Algwinn: «I... forget I said anything. Just... go back to your training, please. I have work to do.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Kel Algwinn: «I... I am too a Sith! I'm more a Sith than you are! I'll show you!!» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Kel Algwinn: «I guess you're right. The dark side is... where I belong. No going back now. Th-thank you.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «Yes... vaguely. The boy burdens himself with doubts and anxieties. It is sad to watch, really, for he does possess a certain flair with the Force. Kel will not survive long. Someone will betray him soon enough, and he will be dead. If you are smart and you know something, that person should be you.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Uthar Wynn: «A student, is it? What might you have to say?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Uthar Wynn: «I see. Then leave me be if you have nothing to offer. I am sure you have much to do.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Uthar Wynn: «Ahhhh. I think I know this one you speak of. You gained his confidence to admit such, did you? I commend your competitive spirit... you have certainly impressed me. I shall speak to the boy straight away.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «Now that's what I like to see... the flash of ambition in a young human's eyes. Your species has such remarkable retinal coloring, did you know that?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Kel Algwinn: «I... don't know. I never really thought about it. Maybe I should seek it out, give it a try. Thank you... thank you so much!» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Kel Algwinn: «Maybe you don't remember me? I'm Kel... Kel Algwinn. We, uh, met in the academy on Korriban.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Kel Algwinn: «I... guess, if that's how you want to look at it. The Sith don't support each other like the Jedi do... and I don't think that's a weakness at all.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «The Jedi would have you believe that peace is a desirable goal. That peace of the spirit is the way the Force is mastered, that a lack of conflict betters man. We know different. It is our passion... our hate and our desire... that fuels the Force. It is conflict that improves the lot of civilization and single being both. Conflict forces one to better oneself. It forces change, growth, adaption... evolution... or death. These are not our laws, but the universe's. Without conflict you have only stagnation.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «What fuels your power with the Force but your passion? The stronger, darker emotions. Anger, hatred, fear... these passions empower us.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «The stronger you become in the Force, the more power you will achieve. But always must you fight for your power. Without strife, your victory has no meaning. Without strife, you do not advance. Without strife, there is only stagnation.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «How many sorts of victories can you imagine? Peaceful victory? Victory by sacrifice? A truce, an achievement? Unless the victory is achieved by demonstrating that your power is superior, it is only an illusion. Temporary at best. We seek more.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «The Force is our servant and our master. Our teacher and our companion. A weapon and a tool. Know it and you know the universe. Master it and you master the universe. Strive for perfection and the Force shall reward you.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «I am not surprised. The Force is strong with you... master it and it shall serve you well.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Uthar Wynn: «"The Force shall free me." Yes, good. You have, indeed, learned the Code of the Sith. Speaking the words and knowing the words are, however, two different things.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Uthar Wynn: «Well done. I will test you no more... you know the Code. Very impressive.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Sith Teacher: «How do you do, hopeful? Seeking prestige, yes?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Sith Teacher: «I am a trainer here at the academy. I think I may be able to offer you some aid in gaining prestige, if you desire.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Sith Teacher: «May the Force serve you well.» [K1/Sith_Academy] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Sith Teacher: «Do you know of our renegade students?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Sith Teacher: «Ah. Let me tell you what I can, then. A group of students were ordered to perform executions on civilians within the colony. They refused to carry out the orders. Master Uthar naturally ordered them killed... but they fled before this could take place. If one could carry out Uthar's order, it would be worth considerable prestige. There are five of them, if I remember... and they cannot be far, as they have no method to leave the planet. Likely hiding in the hills on the surface.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Sith Student: «You simply need to follow the east passage... there's an exit there which will lead you up to the surface. Look for Tariga... she is in that area and can probably help you.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Sith Student: «Isn't that what you're interested in? Although I suppose you could also keep it...» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Sith Student: «I imagine you've got a lot to do.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Shaardan: «Shaardan's the name. Don't forget it. You'll have me to thank when you get kicked out of here.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Shaardan: «You'll get yours. Just you wait.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Shaardan: «The name's Shaardan. Didn't I tell you that back in the colony? Maybe not.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Shaardan: «That's true. I had no idea you were coming for the academy when I met you in the colony, though.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Adrenas: «No, not truly. I serve the Sith, but I do not study and train in the Force as you do.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Adrenas: «You are one of the hopefuls who look to gain prestige, yes? I am afraid there is little that I could help you with. I am sure you would prefer to speak to one of the students, here, or perhaps the trainers.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Adrenas: «Galactic Basic is the language of the Republic, but I come from a world beyond the edges of Republic space. There the Twi'lek language of interstellar trade and commerce is the most common dialect. I can speak your language, but my accent is still very pronounced. Some at the Academy would mock me for this, and I am not eager to become the subject of ridicule. There are many humans here who come from worlds beyond Republic space. We seem to be more sympathetic to the Sith cause. Some of them are even training at the Academy, unlike me.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Adrenas: «If you wish. Good luck with your training.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Sith Teacher: «Hah! I doubt that. Have it your way, however, off you go.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Sith Teacher: «I am one of the academy trainers, of course. You'd do well to watch your tone with me, hopeful... I'll be one of your instructors when and if you reach your final test.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Sith Teacher: «Oh, yes. You gather prestige to earn the opportunity to take the final test... you don't automatically become a student. Heh heh... you'll see.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Sith Teacher: «Hmph. As if I didn't have better things to do. Has anyone told you about Jorak Uln?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Tariga: «Greetings, fellow student. Are you planning on going to the Valley of the Dark Lords? I am headed there soon, myself.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Tariga: «The exit at the end of this hall will bring you to the surface. There you merely follow a natural path through the rock and you will come to the valley, itself. You will find many Sith working on the dig at the southern end of the valley. If you head into the northern end where the strange pillars are, do beware of the tuk'ata beasts.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Tariga: «There are four tombs that have been uncovered so far, each holding the remains of a Sith Lord from a time long ago when the Sith were first on Korriban. We have found evidence that there were larger structures underground, a veritable ancient fortress... but it appears to have been destroyed. The tombs are all that remain intact. Currently we are allowed access only to three tombs... the one furthest to the left may only be entered by Master Uthar and those he brings with him. Fortunately there is still much else for us to find. I am pleased that you have joined us.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Tariga: «Yes, indeed. Shortly before entering the valley, you will pass by a cave entrance. Do not enter it... it is full of dangerous creatures we call shyrack. The only other danger to speak of is the tuk'ata. There are guards that will protect you if you remain in the south part of the valley, however.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Tariga: «I wish you well, fellow student.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Mekel: «Well, you're off to a super start. You've got no more prestige than I do, at the moment. Thing is: I know how to get some.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Mekel: «Think what you like. Just remember: only one of us gets to become a Sith.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Mekel: «Uhhh... no. Nobody here will tell you anything about that. I've got a better plan, though.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Mekel: «You think I would tell you? Have my brains started dribbling out of my nose, or something?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Mekel: «The Valley of the Sith Lords. You know... where the academy does all its excavating. If you really don't know, just go out the east exit and you'll see.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Sith Interrogator: «Just activate the console... it gives you a variety of options for serum dosage. The right amount applied at the right rate should do it. The real trick is that the only way to gauge your progress is to watch the prisoner closely for his reaction. Our scanners can't pick up his vitals.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Sith Victim: «I know your language, Sith - but I won't give you the satisfaction of speaking it!» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Sith Victim: «You... you are trying to trick me! I do not believe you!» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Computer Panel: «[How do you wish to use the console?]» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Computer Panel: «[You step away from the console.]» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Sith Victim: «Never! A Mandalorian will never fall before such as you!» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Sith Interrogator: «Of course not. Information extraction is my specialty... I'm not about to let it be known that some whelp did my job better. You had some fun, didn't you? Now run along and let's not turn this into anything unpleasant.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Sith Interrogator: «Oh? I suppose one would need to be alive to go running to Master Uthar to report the location of the cache, wouldn't they? Does that mean that you are ready to battle a true Sith, then? Shall we make an issue of it?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Uthar Wynn: «Oh? And why would that be of use to me? His interrogator already brought me that information moments ago. A valiant attempt, young one, but you must be quicker than that if you wish to impress me.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Sith Interrogator: «Ha! Gather your anger, then... and let us see who is superior!» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Uthar Wynn: «Oh? How very sad, young one. You should learn to grab your advantages whenever you can.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Uthar Wynn: «Hmm. How ironic that I recently had his vessel scrapped for parts, then. I had assumed it had been thoroughly scanned... apparently not. Still... you have done well in bringing me information that others could not unearth on their own. I am impressed.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Lashowe: «My name is Lashowe. Remember it, for I'll be the one who earns the prestige to move on. You'll have to be satisfied with next year, I suppose.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Lashowe: «Do run along... you have a long way to catch up, after all.» [K1/Sith_Academy] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Lashowe: «A duel? Fine, you run along and get ready while I go gain some prestige. Idiot.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Lashowe: «Just figured that out, did you? It just so happens that I have a sure-fire way of pulling ahead in this little contest of ours. You don't really have a chance.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Lashowe: «And what do you have to brag about? You haven't earned any prestige at all.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Lashowe: «I've found an artifact that will impress Uthar far more than anything you could ever scrounge up. I'll win for sure.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Lashowe: «Hah! Like I would tell you. Why don't I just let you rob me while you're at it?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Jolee: «Now where are you going with this, I wonder?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Lashowe: «Hmph. I suppose I *could* use some help. The artifact I know of certainly won't be easy to take. But why should I work with you?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Lashowe: «[Failure] How gullible to you believe me to be? The furthest tomb? Yeah right. Find your own prestige.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Lashowe: «And I'm just supposed to trust *you*? You're the one who asked to work on this, remember? You watch me and I'll watch you. I don't trust you any more than you trust me. If you don't like that, don't come.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Lashowe: «Because it probably contains secrets from the time when the Sith ruins on this planet first existed. You don't think that Master Uthar would find that impressive?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Sith Duelist: «This is where you get to hone your killing styles. We get sent captives all the time to practice on. It's very handy.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Sith Duelist: «You only get so many before the terminal cuts you off. Some of the students are a little bloodthirsty and get carried away, after all.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Sith Duelist: «Just go to the computer console and open up one of the cages. And don't be wasteful... we only get so many captives a month, you know.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Captive Republic Soldier: «And let you hunt me down? NEVER!!» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Captive Republic Soldier: «You... you're going to make me fight, aren't you? Then... then fight I will!!» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Dustil Onasi: «The name's Dustil. That satisfy your curiosity enough?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Dustil Onasi: «So you know my last name, so what?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Dustil Onasi: «What is with the questions? My life is none of your business, so get lost.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Dustil Onasi: «No, I'm not. I don't know who you're talking about.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Dustil Onasi: «Look... I don't want to hear that name ever again. Ever. You understanding me? Now get lost.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Dustil Onasi: «As if I'm not busy enough with my own work? Go pester someone else with your stupid questions.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Dustil Onasi: «Oh, I'm sorry, did I hurt your feelings? Why don't you run to Master Uthar and tell him all about it. I'm sure he'll be very sympathetic.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Dustil Onasi: «You know what I want? I want for you to run along and do whatever it is you're supposed to be doing. I'm too busy to deal with morons.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Dustil Onasi: «Cute. I wonder how interested Master Uthar would be to know just who he has in his web. Unless you've switched sides, Father? But I doubt that.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
