- Carth: «Stop it, Dustil! Stop it! I won't let you go on like this! What have they done to you?!» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Dustil Onasi: «I'm not going to follow you around, forget it. If you're caught, they'll just think I was betraying them.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Carth: «I have a datapad I want you to look at. You knew someone named Selene?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Carth: «I think so. If he's anything like he used to be, Dustil hates to be tricked. There's no way he'll let the Sith trick him again. As for whether or not he'll be my son again... I don't know. He's so full of anger and hate... I wasn't expecting him to be like this. Maybe we can work it out. I hope so. I guess I'll have to wait and see. Thanks, by the way... for all your help.» [K1/Sith_Academy]

**[K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]**
- Belaya: «So you do not even have the decency to remember me? I have cursed your name every moment since we last met on Dantooine.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- Vrook Lamar: «I find that... disturbing. It is our failings as teachers that enabled this to happen. Do not give in to the same dark urges as she did or you may suffer a similar fate.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- Mekel: «Isn't that obvious? You must be very sharp to possess such an astute sense of perception. Obviously deaf, however, since I asked you to leave.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- Mekel: «No you may not. I'm busy amusing myself and I'm not going to miss any of the fun. Now get lost before I teach you a lesson.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- Prospective Sith Student: «Th-thank you... wish me luck.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- Mekel: «Then I trust you'll leave. I think another one of these fools is about to drop, and I don't want to miss it.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- Prospective Sith Student: «[Failure] You're wrong! I... must wait here to be worthy; you are... just trying to trick me so you will be chosen!» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- Sith Academy Guard: «You must be admitted to the academy. That decision must be made by a Sith who has already been accepted, here. The final decision, however, remains with Master Yuthura. I believe she is currently at the cantina if you wish to seek her out. Now go.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- Sith Academy Guard: «It is the device given to one who has been accepted into the academy, but has not yet proved their worth as a student. If one of the Sith decides you are worthy, you will be given one... which you must then take to Yuthura Ban, who I believe is in the cantina. It is she who decides which hopefuls enter the academy.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- Sith Academy Guard: «I see. And who might your friends be?» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- Sith Academy Guard: «Ah. I trust you will be responsible for ensuring your slaves are not disruptive, then?» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- Sith Academy Guard: «So long as the Sith do not object to them, neither will I. They will be your responsibility, of course.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- Sith Apprentice: «You can't stop me! You first, human, and then she dies!!» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- Prospective Sith: «If I thought I could take it... ahhh, no, never mind. Just go on and quit lording it over me, will you?» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- Prospective Sith: «What does that mean? It means that you've been accepted into the academy. All you have to do is bring that to Yuthura Ban in the cantina. Didn't the Sith who gave you that tell you?» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- Prospective Sith: «No. You're very lucky, that's all I meant.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- Prospective Sith: «Oh really? What do you know, anyway? Get away from me.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- Prospective Sith: «Hmph. Fine. Keep your secrets to yourself. See if I care.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- Yuthura Ban: «You think me a fool? You think I do not recognize a lightsaber when I see it?» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- Yuthura Ban: «I am sure there are plenty of others here who would answer your questions. I am quite busy. So if there is nothing else...?» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- Yuthura Ban: «Who am I? You must not have been on Korriban for very long. Either that or you have been feeling your way about blindly. Luckily for you, I am in a charitable mood. I am Yuthura Ban, second only to Master Uthar of the Sith academy here in Dreshdae. I am the one who decides which few of the many hopefuls who travel here to train actually become a Sith. Why? Is it your desire to enter the academy? Is that why you are here?» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- Yuthura Ban: «Do not be insolent, human. I asked you a direct question, and I will not be trifled with. Now... where did you get this medallion?» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- Yuthura Ban: «I see. How very enterprising of you, though that is not the normal way that we select our students for the academy.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- Yuthura Ban: «We make no apologies for the weak. If you cannot clench your fist and know when the moment comes to strike, there is no place for you amongst us. Of those who come to train, those who are weak return home. If they are both weak and foolish, they die... but it was their choice to come.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- Yuthura Ban: «Hide-bound relics who burden themselves with tradition and with the protection of the weak and ungrateful. They are pitiful and misguided. Why would you take a gift as glorious as the Force and squander it? Weaken yourself deliberately and shackle yourself to outdated mores? Our gift has made us superior. It is our rightful place to rule. How can any deny that? Yet the Jedi do so... and call us 'evil' because we do not.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- Yuthura Ban: «'Corruption' is a word the pitiful use to describe the natural longing for power that they deny. 'Evil' is a word trumpeted by the weak to ease their heart. It is true that the Force exacts a physical toll on some... but not a single one of them has ever regretted it. Anything else you hear is propaganda.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- Yuthura Ban: «You know the name? Malak is the strongest of us, and the strongest always rules... at least until one who is stronger can take it from him. That is our way: survival of the fittest. You are always on guard, always lean for the kill. We promote it, for through this the Sith are stronger.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- Yuthura Ban: «If a Sith has proven to be weak... and if the time is right. The Force rewards the cunning and the mighty. Step up to the challenge if you dare... or turn tail and run.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- Yuthura Ban: «You've heard enough, have you? *chuckle* So what is it to be? Are you here to be a Sith or not?» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- Yuthura Ban: «Oh? That is truly a first. Do as you wish, then, human. I'll not stop you.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- Yuthura Ban: «No? Why might that be? There is something... odd about you, human, that I cannot place...» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- Yuthura Ban: «*chuckle* As you wish. Play your little game, Jedi, if you must. Either way, I sense an intriguing amount of power within you.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- Yuthura Ban: «So one of the others accepted you, even after I did not? You are lucky, human, for I am in a forgiving mood.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]

**[K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]**
- Sith Security Officer: «Your pass-card is out of date! And I don't recognize you at all! We'll get to the bottom of this soon enough!» [K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]
- Shasa: «Ah, I see you are not as foolish as the others I have met. It is good to see that the Jedi Order also realizes the strength of the Sith, and will do what is right when the time comes. If you encounter my father, tell him I will not return to him. I have cast my lot with the Sith and that is all.» [K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]
- Shasa: «You won't find us to be such easy victims! Our Sith Masters have taught us well!» [K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]
- Shasa: «Spare us your lies! The Sith have treated us with nothing but respect and honor! You speak as if we are prisoners here, but we can leave whenever we wish! Our friend Galas chose to leave, and he was returned safely to his home in Ahto City.» [K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]
- Shasa: «So you say. But why should we believe you? We need physical proof, not the words of some Republic sympathizer.» [K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]
- Shasa: «I must apologize for doubting you. The Sith are truly as evil as you have claimed.» [K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]
- Selkath Officer: «Do not be foolish, human! We have the entire armed forces of Manaan at our disposal. You WILL submit to our authority!» [K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]
- Selkath Officer: «Though the Sith Embassy here is considered sovereign territory of the Sith Empire, we have been monitoring an alarming number of weapon discharges and detonations from within the base. Inquiries to the staff of the Embassy yielded no response. It would seem that contact had been cut. Our cameras recorded you entering the base shortly before contact was lost and fighting apparently began. It is the conclusion of the Ahto City Civil Authority that you are responsible for the disturbance here, and you are hereby placed under arrest. You will come with us to await your trial.» [K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]
- Bwa'lass: «The question of your freedom will be up to the court to decide. All I can do as Arbiter is attempt to sway their decision in your favor.» [K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]
- Bwa'lass: «Yes, Arbiter. The one who defends you before the judges of the Selkath people.» [K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]
- Bwa'lass: «While it is true that you do have that right, I would recommend against it. Due to your confinement, you have not had much of a chance to build up evidence to pursue your case. Also, I do not believe you off-worlders truly understand the minds of we Selkath. It would be best if you left your defense up to me.» [K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]
- Bwa'lass: «You have been charged with initiating violence within the Sith Embassy, murdering members of the Ambassadorial Commission of the Sith Empire, and disregarding our own laws regarding violence in Ahto City.» [K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]
- Bwa'lass: «Be that as it may, I doubt the judges share your view. I will endeavor, however, to make them see your point, off-worlder.» [K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]
- Bwa'lass: «I would first like to ask you a few questions regarding the events that led up to your arrest. I have already been given all relevant data on you and your companions, so that can be disregarded. For what reason did you enter the Sith Embassy?» [K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]
- Bwa'lass: «While that is your right, I strongly recommend against it. I am versed in all the necessary particulars of this case and Selkath law, off-worlder. You would do well to heed my advice.» [K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]
- Bwa'lass: «You must share the evidence with me before I can release you and we can proceed to the courtroom.» [K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]
- Bwa'lass: «You off-worlders are not known for your mental prowess. I shall leave your demise up to you.» [K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]
- Judge Naleshekan: «We will not tolerate insolence! You will obey the rules of our court, or you will be summarily found guilty!» [K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]
- Judge Shelkar: «What is it, human? You must have a good reason for interrupting our proceedings.» [K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]
- Judge Shelkar: «Well... Then I suppose we have no choice but to sentence you for your crimes.» [K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]
- Judge Kota: «Indeed? Then your Arbiter does not speak for you?» [K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]
- Judge Naleshekan: «The court does not dispense in mercy, only justice! Your client will be sentenced according to our laws and tradition!» [K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]
- Bwa'lass: «Is this true? Do you not wish to retain my services?» [K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]
- Judge Naleshekan: «You were clearly seen entering the Sith base. You cannot deny this fact!» [K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]
- Judge Duula: «This mystical darkside you Jedi keep speaking of is a convenient excuse. Basically, they wished to hire you away from the Republic.» [K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Judge Jhosa: «You... attacked them?!» [K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]
- Judge Duula: «You were working for the Republic? So the Republic had you break in and kill the Sith? It is as I suspected...» [K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]
- Judge Naleshekan: «You admit that you entered the Sith base illegally and attempted to commit crimes there! It is obvious that they tried to stop you and that you initiated hostilities!» [K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]
- Judge Naleshekan: «You admit to your crime!» [K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]
- Judge Shelkar: «For the crimes you have committed against the Sith and against the laws of the Ahto City Civil Authority, this court finds no alternative: We hereby sentence you to death. You will be executed immediately. This court has spoken.» [K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]
- Judge Jhosa: «You believe that the Sith have had something to do with those disappearance?» [K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]
- Judge Naleshekan: «You will need to come up with a better excuse than that, off-worlder!» [K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]
- Sith Droid: «What?! How did you... Um... This unit has no knowledge of what you're talking about.» [K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]

**[K1/Sith_Base_(Taris)]**
- Receptionist: «[Success] 50 credits? You've got a deal! The Sith have made my life a living hell ever since they took over this base.» [K1/Sith_Base_(Taris)]
- Receptionist: «You know, it's about time someone stood up to these Sith! Just do me a favour and wait until I'm out of here before you start blasting the place up.» [K1/Sith_Base_(Taris)]
- Receptionist: «You shouldn't threaten a girl who's got an alarm right at her desk!» [K1/Sith_Base_(Taris)]
- Duros: «I suppose all of us Duros look alike to you, human. In any case, it doesn't matter whether you remember helping me before or not.» [K1/Sith_Base_(Taris)]
- Duros: «I have nothing to offer you, other than my gratitude. Please, human, have pity on me.» [K1/Sith_Base_(Taris)]
- Duros: «No, human, please! I beg you not to leave me here. Just set all the panels on the wall to the red "off" position to release me. Is that so much to ask?» [K1/Sith_Base_(Taris)]
- Duros: «Is there nothing I can say to persuade you, human? Please, have pity on me. Reconsider, I beg you.» [K1/Sith_Base_(Taris)]
- Duros: «Switch all the panels on the wall to the red "off" position and I will be released. It is not as simple as it sounds – switching a panel will also switch the panel beside it. You must be careful as you do this, human. If you set all the panels to the green "on" position my cell's termination program will engage and I will be executed.» [K1/Sith_Base_(Taris)]
- Canderous: «Fair enough. But don't take too long. As soon as you're ready to get inside Davik's estate, come speak to me. The sooner we get off this rock the better.» [K1/Sith_Base_(Taris)]
- Canderous: «Davik's always looking to recruit new talent. I'll tell him how you won that swoop race and mention that you're interested in working for the Exchange. I'll say I brought you in so he could check you out. He'll have you stay at his estate for a couple days while he runs some background checks on you – that's standard procedure.» [K1/Sith_Base_(Taris)]

**[K1/South_Apartments]**
- Duros: «Forgive me, human. I only wanted to thank you, I meant no harm. I will leave you in peace.» [K1/South_Apartments]
- Janitor: «Oh, I get it! You figure that just because I'm a janitor you don't have to show me any respect. I'm beneath you, is that it?» [K1/South_Apartments]
- Janitor: «Hmph! You'll change your tune the first time your plumbing breaks down and raw sewage floods your apartment. Then you'll be beggin' for Kadir to come help you out!» [K1/South_Apartments]
- Janitor: «Don't know how much I can tell you, but seeing as how you're new here I guess I can try and help you out.» [K1/South_Apartments]
- Janitor: «Decent enough place to live, I guess. Except for the lower levels. That's where the swoop gangs hang out. Just stay in the Upper City and you'll be fine. You also might want to check on the cantina. I used to go there when I was younger. Good place to get a drink and find out what's going on in the Upper City.» [K1/South_Apartments]
- Janitor: «Well, the best place to go is probably Kebla Yurt's store. She's right across from the cantina, so you can stop in for a drink if you get thirsty while you're shopping.» [K1/South_Apartments]
- Janitor: «Not much of an issue for me, you know? I ain't never left Taris and I ain't never going to. A lot of other folks is sure riled up about it, but I don't really know much about it. I prefer to keep to myself, mostly. But there's plenty of people at the cantina who'll be more than happy to tell you what they think about the Sith and the quarantine.» [K1/South_Apartments]
- Janitor: «I should get back to work anyway. I'll probably see you around – I'm here most of the time. This building doesn't clean itself, you know.» [K1/South_Apartments]
- Janitor: «I'm Kadir. I'm the janitor here! Geez, I know you're a new tenant and all but I didn't think you'd forget me already! I have feelings, you know!» [K1/South_Apartments]
- Janitor: «Yeah, okay then. No hard feelings. So, what can I do for you?» [K1/South_Apartments]
- Janitor: «Hey, I do the best I can with the equipment I got! You're just like all the rest, complaining about me all the time.» [K1/South_Apartments]
- Dia: «You're as bad as that pig Holdan! I'll stay out of your way, but you're wasting your time. I don't have anything worth stealing.» [K1/South_Apartments]
- Dia: «That's no excuse. You can't just go around barging into people's apartments because you're curious! But at least you're more polite than that pig, Holdan.» [K1/South_Apartments]
- Dia: «I… I don't want to talk about it. I'm in enough trouble already. Besides, I don't know if I can trust you.» [K1/South_Apartments]
- Dia: «I can't help you there. Since this thing with Holdan I've been scared to leave my apartment. I don't know anything about what's going on in the outside world anymore.» [K1/South_Apartments]
- Dia: «[Success] Well, I suppose you seem like an alright sort. When I cut Holdan it made him back off, but it also embarrassed him in front of his friends.» [K1/South_Apartments]
- Dia: «I doubt it. Holdan is one of Davik's men. When you work for the local crime lord the authorities tend to turn a blind eye. I'm afraid this is between me and Holdan now.» [K1/South_Apartments]
- Dia: «You could try, I guess. He usually hangs out at the cantina in the Lower City. It probably won't do any good. Holdan's used to getting his own way. That's one of the fringe benefits of being a goon for Davik. Working for the local crime lord let's you get away with things. Still, I appreciate the offer.» [K1/South_Apartments]
- Dia: «Goodbye, and good luck. I hope you can talk some sense into Holdan.» [K1/South_Apartments]
- Dia: «He… he called the bounty off? That's wonderful! I only wish I had something to give you as a reward. Wait a minute... I know! I don't have many credits, but there is something I can give you. It's a family heirloom. It's not much, but it's something.» [K1/South_Apartments]
- Dia: «If I had more I'd gladly give it to you, but this is all I've got. I'm sorry.» [K1/South_Apartments]
- Dia: «Wait! Think about what you're doing. If that pig had just kept his hands to himself, I wouldn't have had to give him the scar with my vibroblade. He gets drunk and tries to force himself on me, and I get a bounty put on my head for defending myself? Where's the justice in that? Holdan's the one to blame, not me.» [K1/South_Apartments]
- Carth: «What's wrong with you? She doesn't deserve this!» [K1/South_Apartments]
- Dia: «Then you're going to learn the same lesson I taught Holdan: I'm not as helpless as I look!» [K1/South_Apartments]
- Larrim: «Oh, okay. Well then, you might be interested in knowing I have one for sale. It isn't cheap, but it could be the difference between life and death.» [K1/South_Apartments]
- Larrim: «By law, most aliens aren't allowed to have a residence in the Upper City of Taris. They allow a few of us Twi'leks up here, but for the most part it's humans only. But no self-respecting Upper City human would want to live in the apartments in this dump. So the landlord has been renting out the apartments illegally to aliens. Better than letting them sit empty, I guess. Otherwise you get squatters and people hiding out from the authorities living in the abandoned apartments, and they don't pay rent.» [K1/South_Apartments]
- Larrim: «The Taris authorities pretty much turned a blind eye to this place. A few illegal aliens just weren't worth the bother. And once the Sith took over, they felt the same way. Besides, I don't live here myself. I just run a small business providing supplies to the residents. You want to see what I have in stock?» [K1/South_Apartments]
- Larrim: «Questions? I'd rather not. It might be bad for business if people saw me answering a bunch of questions for a human. Most of the residents here are illegal aliens, questions are frowned on. I don't want to lose the trust of my customers. If you really need information, go talk to [[../South Apartments#Janitor» [K1/South_Apartments]
- Larrim: «See you around. Come on back if you need any equipment. I've got some merchandise you might be interested in.» [K1/South_Apartments]

**[K1/South_Beach]**
- Hologram: «We are the Elders... and you will answer our question! Who are you and why have you come?» [K1/South_Beach]
- Hologram: «From the Rakata? Then you are a spy and must be killed!» [K1/South_Beach]

**[K1/Star_Forge_System]**
- Carth: «You're right! That Sith fleet we saw must have some type of protection against the disruptor field. We have to find a way to disable it or the Republic will be slaughtered!» [K1/Star_Forge_System]
- Jolee: «I am glad to hear you say that. Now that you know your true identity I was afraid you might slip back over to the dark side. If Bastila feels as you do there may yet be hope for her.» [K1/Star_Forge_System]
- Carth: «Oh. I had... hoped that there might be. I don't blame you. Uh. You're not the same woman I met on Taris anymore, are you? Well, thank you for your honesty. I still intend to see this through to the end, whatever happens. Malak needs to be stopped. And uh, you... you'll make the decision you have to. And we'll have to deal with that day when it comes.» [K1/Star_Forge_System]
- Carth: «Whatever's happened up until this point, there's going to come a time very soon where you're going to have to make a choice. And there won't be any turning back. I want you to make the right choice. I want to give you a reason to.» [K1/Star_Forge_System]
- Canderous: «This world is... odd. It looks like a battlefield, but the environment is lush and green. Whatever happened here was a long time ago. I have seen many strange worlds in my time, but this world is stranger still. I don't like this place. Something happened... or is happening here and I don't like it. We should get out of here as soon as possible. You have anything else you want to ask?» [K1/Star_Forge_System]

**[K1/Starboard_Section]**
- Carth: «First, make sure you have a stealth field generator equipped on your belt. You can't use your stealth skill without one. {{control» [K1/Starboard_Section]
- Carth: «Be careful! There's a whole squad of Sith Troopers on the other side of that door! You need to find some way to thin their numbers. You could reprogram the damaged assault droid to help you, if you have enough repair [[../Utility items#Parts» [K1/Starboard_Section]
- Combat Droid: «You leave the droid alone for now.» [K1/Starboard_Section]
- Carth: «I'm a soldier with the Republic, like you. We’re the last two crew members left on the Endar Spire. Bastila's escape pod's already gone, so there's no reason for us to stick around here and get shot by the Sith. Now come on – there'll be time for questions later!» [K1/Starboard_Section]

**[K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]**
- Elora: «Someone is out to destroy my husband. I... I don't know who I can trust. I don't know you. But Jolee - he was always a true friend to Sunry.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Elora: «Please, I beg you to bring Jolee to speak to me about my husband. Sunry's life depends on it!» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Judge Shelkar: «That is acceptable, I believe. In Manaan legal tradition the defendant can have an Arbiter who tries to prove his innocence in court. This Arbiter is a neutral party that can investigate and present evidence in the trial, and argue on the defendant's behalf. As you are a Jedi, I believe you could fulfill this role adequately.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Judge Kota: «Sunry was a great warrior in his time. He fought for the Republic against the Sith aggression and proved valorous. I have great respect for his accomplishments and find it tragic that he finds himself in his current condition. If the Republic had more individuals like him right now, I do not believe the Sith would be so great a problem for your people. Is there any other way I can assist you?» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Judge Kota: «I find it questionable that a man with such a reputable past as Sunry would murder a Sith woman. Especially not from behind. But knowing the Sith, there was probably some deception involved. They seek to do the same thing to us here. There has been much pressure by the Sith on our government - and even on the judges themselves - to deliver a guilty verdict in this case. But the letter of the law is absolute, and we will not be compromised for Sith power-games. Is there anything else you need?» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Judge Kota: «I find this great battle being waged across the galaxy both disturbing and, ultimately, tragic. For all its faults, your tottering Republic has benefitted the vast majority of the galaxy for many millenia. To see it fall into ruin in such a short time... I do not like the Sith, that I make no effort to hide. I will even admit that if given the option, I would rule that we should join the Republic. But I must remain impartial and rule on this case as the law requires.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Judge Kota: «If there is anything else you require, feel free to inquire with me.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Judge Shelkar: «You have the right, Arbiter, to ask me questions regarding the case.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Judge Shelkar: «Sunry is known to us as a hero of the Republic in your perpetual conflicts with the Sith Empire. While he may have been severely injured in the line of duty, I personally hold him in quite high regard.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Judge Shelkar: «The governing bodies of both states seek to keep an amicable relationship with our world in order to facilitate the exporting of kolto. I do not delude myself into thinking that either of your states holds any great love for us, but we are necessary so you will let us hold our autonomy. I try not to concern myself with intragalactic policy. I find it stretches the letter of the law too much for my tastes. Is there something else that concerns you, human?» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Judge Shelkar: «What is it you wish to ask?» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Judge Shelkar: «Very well. When you feel you have gathered enough evidence, you may speak to the warden in charge of the prison where Sunry is being held. He will bring Sunry to us and the trial will commence.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Judge Naleshekan: «Sunry was a Republic soldier, a hero even. Do you think his Republic masters would let someone so valuable go so easily? I think not. I think Sunry maintains his contacts with the Republic and seeks to escalate the situation here. Is there something else you require?» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Judge Naleshekan: «I do not buy into the Republic line that this is some epic battle of the 'good' Republic against the 'evil' Sith Empire. The Sith empire is merely an expansionistic power, much like the Republic was early in its history. It is simply the turning of history, where the old is swept away by the better new. Is there anything intelligent you wish to know?» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Judge Naleshekan: «Then go and do not disturb me unless you have trial business to discuss.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Judge Jhosa: «Sunry is a former Republic officer who is highly regarded by your people. He is admired by your people here on Manaan, and even seems to maintain some sort of relations with them still. If he is as capable as some of the Republic citizens still believe him to be, he is certainly capable of actions that would result in this murder. Is there anything else you wish to ask in relation to this case?» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Judge Jhosa: «Well I have often wondered, if Sunry were so innocent, why was he seen fleeing the scene of the crime? Why would he have been running if he had not committed the crime himself, or was at least aware of what had occurred? This case seems fairly simple and obvious to me. You Republic types stick together, however, and I assume you will go to lengths to defend him.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Judge Jhosa: «Return if you have pertinent details about the case to discuss.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Jolee: «Hmph. I'm a Jedi... and one who was living alone in the Shadowlands until recently. That keeps you in shape, damn it! We're talking about Sunry, here.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Elora: «Lies! It... it must be! I knew the Sith would never let a Republic hero get away... You must find Sunry innocent!» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Gonto Vas: «What do you expect when a Sith spy and a Republic war hero are having an affair? Anyone could see it would end ugly.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Gonto Vas: «Don't know much else. If I did you can bet I'd be blackmailing either the Sith or the Republic to keep quiet.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Gonto Vas: «Oh-ho! So, you didn't know about Sunry stepping out on his wife? Well it may have been a secret to some, but I'm not blind! I should have charged you for that juicy tidbit, but I figured anyone investigating the murder would have already figured that out by now. Okay - you got a freebie out of me. But that's all I know about Sunry and his hot Sith side dish.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Sunry: «Just like old times, eh Jolee? You come swooping in out of nowhere to save my tail from the fire.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Sunry: «Well, I guess if you're going to be my Arbiter you'll need to hear what happened. I might as well start at the beginning.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Sunry: «Go ahead, ask... ask what you need to. I've got nothing to hide.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Sunry: «A Rodian? I didn't know there were any Rodians on Manaan. Oh, except for Tyvark... and this one named Gluupor who was hanging around the hotel all the time. OK, so maybe there are a whole bunch of Rodians on Manaan, but what does that matter? If he planted evidence at the scene it's a clear sign of a frame-up. He probably took all the evidence of the real killer at the same time. The evidence of a Sith conspiracy keeps getting stronger, doesn't it? But I suspect there is still stuff you want to clear up.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Sunry: «How would they know? They weren't there! I heard some of the witnesses said that too, but I swear she was alive when I left! I wonder if the Sith have been putting pressure on people to get them to convict me... It wouldn't surprise me at all. They've had it in for me since the war... You should ask everyone about that... they might admit to helping the Sith under pressure.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Sunry: «Er... OK. What else do you want to know?» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Sunry: «I guess you could interview witnesses at the hotel where the murder happened. The Selkath already did that, but the fish folk don't have much rapport with off-worlders. Maybe one of them was holding something back. But I think you'd be better off investigating the Sith themselves. Maybe you could find some proof they planted evidence to frame me - though that might mean figuring out a way into the Sith Embassy.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Sunry: «Well I was at the hotel, I admit. And I did meet Elassa there that night. But it's not what you think! I... I was working with Republic Intelligence. We were pretending to feed information to the Sith while I turned Elassa into a double agent working for us. It was going well, too... Until she turned up dead... Hm... maybe the Sith had found out I was turning her and had her eliminated...» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Sunry: «Very well. I suppose there is still much you must do before the trial. Once you've gotten enough evidence to go to trial just tell the warden we're ready. The sooner I get out of this cell the better.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Sunry: «So the truth is out. I never meant to kill her, you know. When I found out she was a Sith spy using me to gain information, something inside me just snapped. She didn't know I had found out, so I waited until she fell asleep and then I... I killed her. Simple, really. But once I realized what I'd done I... I panicked. I contacted those spies from the Republic to help me. They found the illegal monitoring device that the Sith had planted in the room, altered its recording to hide my part in it and cleared up the evidence. I figured that was the end of it.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Sunry: «It's easy for you to judge me - but you weren't there! You don't know what it was like, realizing what I'd done. But it...but it doesn't matter anyway.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Sunry: «The Sith must have been spying on me and Elassa, trying to make sure she wasn't a double agent. They must have seen the Republic spies getting rid of all the evidence. And so they planted their own evidence. Elassa must have stolen that Hero's Cross from me long ago and given it to her Sith masters, like some kind of trophy. I just thought I'd misplaced it. But when the Cross turned up on her body, I knew the Sith had put it there. So now you know the whole story, what are you going to do?» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Sunry: «You think I'm some kind of monster, don't you? All I did was kill a Sith! How many Sith have you killed? Dozens? Hundreds? Thousands?» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Jolee: «But Sunry is a murderer. I'm sorry, old friend, but you are. I... I can't approve of this. You go ahead, however, if that's what you think is best.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Selkath Warden: «Yes, actually I have. We find it very suspicious, but there are no signs of kidnappings. It is likely, though, that it has been perpetrated by out-worlders... ones such as yourself perhaps...» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Selkath Warden: «Yes, I am aware of this fact, human. He is in his cage over there and I am sure can hear you quite clearly.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Selkath Warden: «Are you certain, human? Once the trial has begun it cannot be stopped. And once judgment is given, the case can never be reopened. You must be certain your investigations are complete before you request a trial. This is the role of the Arbiter. Are you certain you wish to begin the trial now?» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Selkath Warden: «Very well then. I will, of course, be here if you need something else.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Sunry: «No! You traitor!» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Judge Shelkar: «If you attempt such manipulations again, Sunry will be found guilty, and you will summarily be put to death! We do not tolerate intrusions upon the justice of our courts!» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Judge Shelkar: «Objection sustained. Ask the witness your questions and do it promply.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Firith Me: «You mean actually see him kill her with my own eyes? Well... no... But I don't think there could have been anyone else there.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Firith Me: «I think it may have been. I mean, you'd have to pretty stupid to leave something that important lying around, wouldn't you?» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Elora: «Yes. He fought against the Sith in the last war. He fought bravely and was even awarded the Hero's Cross! I don't know how much that means to the Sith or to your Selkath, your Honors, but in the Republic it is awarded only to the bravest, most valorous, most honorable men. The kind of man who earns that medal would not kill someone in cold blood!» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Judge Shelkar: «Very well. Would you like to use this opportunity to poll the judges?» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Jolee: «You defended him well enough. I wish we had had time to look into matters more fully, but wishing is only useful for the young and idealistic. What's done is done. The Republic will likely appeal the matter eventually and he'll be free. I just... I just would like to know if that's best. Sunry was a good man once. And a good friend. I'll miss him. Bah. I don't want to talk about this anymore. My jaw aches.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Jolee: «You did what was necessary. Sunry was guilty, as sad as that fact makes me, justice had to be done. Doesn't matter that his victim was a Sith or that he was once a hero. I only wish that my old friend hadn't done it. But that's the thing about wishes. They don't come true. And now Sunry's dead, which is a shame. He *was* a good man, you know... once. Bah. I don't want to talk about this anymore. My jaw aches.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Jolee: «Innocent, mm? I don't know what to make of it, to tell the truth. Do you think justice has been done?» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]

**[K1/Swoop_Platform]**
- Mechanic: «Normally a rider can run as many heats as they want, but I don't know how long the prototype accelerator on your swoop will hold up. No more than four or five races, I'd guess. So make your runs count.» [K1/Swoop_Platform]
- Mechanic: «Sounds like you're ready. Go talk to the race announcer over by the counter. He'll give you the time to beat and get you out on the course. I'll be waiting for you here after the run so I can make any tweaks or repairs to your swoop in case you want to go out for another heat. Good luck. And don't worry about the prototype overheating and exploding while you're out on the track. That *probably* won't happen.» [K1/Swoop_Platform]
- Mechanic: «Sure, no problem. Avoid the obstacles on the track by veering your swoop left or right. Any rocks or debris you hit will slow you down. Every time your swoop passes over one of the accelerator pads you'll get a boost of speed, so try to hit them. And remember to switch gears when you see your engine revs climbing.» [K1/Swoop_Platform]
- Mechanic: «Good to hear. Go talk to the race announcer and he'll get you out on the track. I'll be waiting for you here after the run so I can make any tweaks or repairs to your swoop in case you want to go out for another heat. Good luck. And don't worry about the prototype overheating and exploding while you're out on the track. That *probably* won't happen.» [K1/Swoop_Platform]
- Mechanic: «Look, we're running out of time. That accelerator's one step away from being scrap metal. You've got to take some risks if you want to collect the champion's prize!» [K1/Swoop_Platform]
- Mechanic: «I don't think you'll back out now. Word is you've got an unhealthy interest in that female Republic officer Brejik's offering as a prize. The only way you'll get her is if you put up the best time. So quit sulking and start racing like you mean it!» [K1/Swoop_Platform]
- Mechanic: «That's what I like to hear! Just remember, this is your last chance. That accelerator won't hold together for another race... we'll be lucky if it doesn't explode in this one! But don't you go worrying about that. You just stay focused on that finish line. Good luck... we're counting on you.» [K1/Swoop_Platform]
- Race Announcer: «Okay. Just come back and see me when you're ready to hit the track.» [K1/Swoop_Platform]
- Race Announcer: «Same as usual – he was pushing himself beyond his limits and he hit some debris on the track. The deflectors kept him from crashing, but the force of the impact jarred him from his swoop. Don't worry... they scraped what was left of him off the track already. Just remember to hang on and you should be okay. You can hit the track whenever you're ready.» [K1/Swoop_Platform]
- Race Announcer: «Okay, let's get you out on the track.» [K1/Swoop_Platform]
- Brejik: «When you hear what I have to say you'll understand. It seems the woman I offered up as a prize is more than just a Republic officer – she's actually a Jedi! I've had to equip her with a neural disruptor collar to keep her mind in a state of unfocused confusion just to make sure she doesn't use her Force powers to escape. Obviously, I can't turn her over to you – it would be far too dangerous to have a Jedi as a slave. But rest assured, I'll find some other way to compensate you for your victory.» [K1/Swoop_Platform]
- Bastila: «Save me? Is that what you were trying to accomplish by riding in that swoop race? Well, as far as rescues go this is a pretty poor example. In case you hadn't noticed, I managed to free myself from that neural restraint collar without your help. In fact, it's more accurate to say that I saved you! Brejik and his Vulkars would have left you for dead if I hadn't stepped into that fight. You're lucky I was here to get you out of this mess!» [K1/Swoop_Platform]
- Bastila: «I may have been a prisoner, but a Jedi is never helpless. Maybe you've heard of a little thing called 'the Force'? But I suppose I shouldn't be too hard on you. You did try to save me after all, even if it didn't go quite as planned. So let's get down to business – we're not out of danger yet.» [K1/Swoop_Platform]
- Bastila: «The Republic, for one. I was fleet commander, remember? But I don't want you getting all worked up over nothing. So tell me – do you have a plan to get off this planet?» [K1/Swoop_Platform]
- Bastila: «Carth Onasi is alive? Finally, some good news! Carth is one of the Republic's best soldiers. He's proved himself a hero a dozen times over! And he sent you here to save me? Maybe I misjudged you. Carth wouldn't have sent you if he wasn't confident in your... abilities. Forgive me – despite my Jedi training, I still tend to act a bit rashly sometimes.» [K1/Swoop_Platform]
- Bastila: «I see. Now that I'm back in charge of this mission, perhaps we can start doing things properly. Hopefully our escape from Taris will go more smoothly than when you "rescued" me from Brejik.» [K1/Swoop_Platform]
- Bastila: «Yes... You're right, of course. I apologize, Carth. This has been a difficult time for me. Of course I'm happy to listen to your advice. What do you suggest we do?» [K1/Swoop_Platform]
- Twi'lek: «No, human. I don't think so. I saw the swoop race: you were there. You posted the best time. You are the one I was sent to find.» [K1/Swoop_Platform]
- Twi'lek: «Yes, that's the one. Given his connections, you can see why it would be wise to attend this meeting.» [K1/Swoop_Platform]
- Twi'lek: «A wise choice, human. I would advise you not to wait too long. Mandalorians are known for many things, but patience is not one of them.» [K1/Swoop_Platform]
- Canderous: «Hold on just a second. Believe me, you'll want to hear this.» [K1/Swoop_Platform]
- Canderous: «I've got a plan to escape Taris, but I can't do it alone. I need someone I know can get the job done to help me. That's where you come in.» [K1/Swoop_Platform]
- Canderous: «Here's the deal: you bring me those launch codes and I can provide the vehicle to get off the planet – Davik's flagship, the Ebon Hawk!» [K1/Swoop_Platform]
- Canderous: «Uh-uh. Not yet. First, you bring me the launch codes. Then I'll tell you the rest of my plan.» [K1/Swoop_Platform]
- Canderous: «Good luck – you're going to need it. The entrance to the base is sealed tight.» [K1/Swoop_Platform]
- Canderous: «Look, I don't like working with strangers either but we don't have much choice. I can provide the ship, you can get me those codes. We need each other.» [K1/Swoop_Platform]
- Janice Nall: «Oh, Canderous sent you! Well why didn't you say so? Okay, the droid is ready. You can have it for 2000 credits.» [K1/Swoop_Platform]
- Janice Nall: «You're joking, right? You mean to tell me Canderous sent you to collect this droid for Davik and you can't pay? I find that hard to believe. But it's not my problem, is it? So... are you going to pay?» [K1/Swoop_Platform]
- Janice Nall: «Okay, that's your decision I guess. Seems to me Canderous wouldn't have sent you if he didn't want you to buy it, though. So, is there anything else I can do for you?» [K1/Swoop_Platform]
- Janice Nall: «He gave me a down payment, but the rest is due on delivery. If he sent you here to get the droid without giving you the final payment, that's between you and him. But I still need my 2000 credits.» [K1/Swoop_Platform]
- Janice Nall: «[Failure] Half? Are you kidding? This is *the* best droid you'll ever buy! The price is non-negotiable, even for one of Davik's agents. The price is firm – 2000 credits, take it or leave it.» [K1/Swoop_Platform]
- Janice Nall: «Wow... look at you, big spender! Okay, the T3-M4 unit is yours. Pleasure doing business with you. A real pleasure.» [K1/Swoop_Platform]
- Janice Nall: «[Success] Hey, I don't want trouble with Davik. Look, take the droid, he's yours. But the next time Davik wants me to do some custom work, he's out of luck!» [K1/Swoop_Platform]

**[K1/Swoop_Registration]**
- Motta the Hutt: «That's great. Except I don't care. Let me rephrase the important part of my question. Do you own your own swoop?» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Motta the Hutt: «Ha! You and twelve other liars. Planet blows up, race records are lost, and now everyone is a champion!» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Motta the Hutt: «Yeah, you do that. I've got business to work on. Big business!» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Motta the Hutt: «You got a bike, then you can race. I don't care about experience, so long as you can get out on the track. And if you crash and burn, that's your problem. I just need warm bodies to pad the roster between the pros. Err... there is the matter of a small track maintenance fee... 100 credits.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Motta the Hutt: «Then you don't race! I pay out good money to encourage competition. I need to make it back somehow.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Motta the Hutt: «If you don't have the confidence to win back the money you give me, why should I care if you race or not! Bah! Pay it or leave, that's the deal.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Motta the Hutt: «Good! That shows you mean business. Now you can use the track for recreational runs, or I can give you an official race right away.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Motta the Hutt: «All right, but don't make me regret giving you track access. I need racers that race!» [K1/Swoop_Registration] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Nico Senvi: «Racer stuff. Nothing you'd understand. Don't mean to be rude, but it's kind of secret.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Nico Senvi: «Ha! Sure! Race your bike out of that explosion? Everyone knows that the whole planet is gone. Nobody got out. I'm not in the mood for jokes, thanks. I've got things to think about. You wouldn't know anything about it.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Motta the Hutt: «How does... 20 racing bonds sound? That should cover all your swoop costs on Tatooine nicely. I'll make it all back if he signs the deal. I know it.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Motta the Hutt: «Racing bonds are monetary notes I issue so local vendors know who my friends are. Any vendor on Tatooine will buy them. They are worth ten times their number in credits, and sometimes more. It depends on how much of a friend you are.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Motta the Hutt: «He'll be great one day, I can tell. There are some pros here, but I'm not looking for experience. I want potential. Besides, he's young. He won't give me trouble like the older riders, or someone like you.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Motta the Hutt: «No, no, you are a representative, and you'll be his friend. Let him know what the right choice is.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Nico Senvi: «You have to step up and do it to know anything about it. You'll understand once you post some fast times.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Nico Senvi: «Just a few years. I used to run down in the canyons, but nobody sees you down there. I wanted crowds.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Nico Senvi: «Motta has some basic incentives for good times, which you probably know, but this is just an amateur track. The pro circuit is where the real credits are, but even that is hurting. I guess a lot of riders were on Taris.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Nico Senvi: «All right. See you around the track.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Motta the Hutt: «You're not going to pull that typical swoop-jockey attitude, are you? This is business, and I know what's best. What do you care whether I make money off the kid or not? He wants to race and you'll get paid. We all win.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Motta the Hutt: «Anchorhead is a safe town. I don't think you'll be able to do much of anything to me around here.» [K1/Swoop_Registration] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Nico Senvi: «Did you? I'm not sure that it's any of your business. Top racers keep to their own. I don't know you. Maybe if you were better on the track I might feel like talking about it. Someone who can turn out the fast times might understand.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Nico Senvi: «You did? I don't believe it. I mean, I do, but I sure wasn't expecting it. I appreciate it. I really do.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Nico Senvi: «Hard to tell. I'll get some more runs here first. I need the practice. I have to top *your* time.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Nico Senvi: «You've posted some good times... he might listen to you. I would. I respect you as a racer. But... I kind of doubt he'd give me a better deal. He doesn't have to. He is putting up most of the money.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Nico Senvi: «Do you now? You might know a good deal, I guess. You sure know your way around the track.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Motta the Hutt: «Yes, the contract trouble I've been having. Have you talked to the boy? You don't get paid until he signs.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Motta the Hutt: «We had a deal. It was fair and I don't see why you need more.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Motta the Hutt: «Eh? What's this? A box? Oh... *that* box. Yeah, I know what box you're talking about. I thought that was supposed to be here a month ago. You take a detour, huh? Ahh, never mind. You open the box?» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Motta the Hutt: «[Failure] And I expect my goods to be delivered on time. You have a problem with the price, you talk to Lurze.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Garm Totryl: «Well sure, but I don't know much besides the track. You even interested in the swoop track?» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Garm Totryl: «Heck, nobody is ever interested in me. I'm the new guy, Garm Totryl. Pleased to meet you.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Garm Totryl: «Ha! You're kidding, right? Every racer here would rather be someplace else, but this is it for most of us. Taris blowing up has messed up the swoop circuit. People are just looking for somewhere to keep their skills sharp. It sounds weird, doesn't it? Worrying about racing when all those people died? Nothing we can do.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Garm Totryl: «Motta pays for timekeeping and official records. You can race anywhere, but here, if you do good, it actually matters. That's why I stick around. No work, no future, but Tatooine at least has this. It's my shot... maybe.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Garm Totryl: «How they keep their nerves steady, bike maintenance. You know, all of it. I'm not as good as some, but I try.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Garm Totryl: «You should ask them yourself. I don't feel right speaking for the pros. I'm the new guy to them.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Garm Totryl: «Anyone but us swoop racers. Really, we can show you the track, but anything else, we've got no clue. You could check with the Czerka office. They like to think they know everything.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Garm Totryl: «[Success] [His face drops. Clearly he has just realized that you are a very, very dangerous person.]» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Yuka Rill: «If I say no you'll probably hound me to death. Fine, what do you want?» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Yuka Rill: «There's not much useful you'll get out of me. Yuka Rill is on the way down. I can't match the young riders.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Yuka Rill: «He doesn't fear the track. You get old, you start to worry about failing. He just lets it all come to him.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Yuka Rill: «You don't look like anyone I owe. Stop fooling with me.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Yuka Rill: «Go on, I've seen your type before. Go push the kids around. They don't put up much of a fight.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Zoriis Bafka: «My name is Zoriis Bafka. I suppose I am one of the more experienced racers here. What do you want me to answer?» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Zoriis Bafka: «You lose the skills if you don't use them. I could race on any open road, but competition gives the push to get better. Besides, if my presence lends a little prestige to the track, maybe we'll get some new racers out of it.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Zoriis Bafka: «If you wish. I don't mind answering.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Zoriis Bafka: «Racer's luck be with you.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Zoriis Bafka: «The humor of this is lost on me. The mood on this planet is harsh enough without your games. Now, if we are through, I must get back to my race preparations. I prefer to leave your unpleasantness behind.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Zoriis Bafka: «Is that all? Then take what I have. Yes, I'm just giving it to you. I can afford to turn this mugging into simple beggary.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]

**[K1/Swoop_racing]**
- Mechanic: «Hold on... I better explain the rules to you. Each rider goes out on the track alone. At the end of the day, whoever posted the fastest time is the winner.» [K1/Swoop_racing]
- Track Coordinator: «We keep tabs on all active racers. Currently we have information on you, Garm Totryl, Yuka Rill, and Zoriis Bafka. Nico has withdrawn for the time being.» [K1/Swoop_racing]
- Track Coordinator: «Nothing yet. You'll have to put in some track time before we can record your results.» [K1/Swoop_racing]
- Track Coordinator: «Just don't keep me too long. Other racers might want to have a run down the track. Do you want a race?» [K1/Swoop_racing]
- Track Coordinator: «You'll have to go through Motta for that. He keeps track of all official standings at this track. There's money in it too. He pays riders for good times to encourage competition.» [K1/Swoop_racing]
- Track Coordinator: «Very good. We'll have your bike put in position for the next time trial. Get ready, it won't take long.» [K1/Swoop_racing]
- Motta the Hutt: «Yes, you'll be wanting to race. I'm sure you'll give the swoop fans a good show.» [K1/Swoop_racing]
- Motta the Hutt: «Tier one here is strictly amateurs. Might get a good race out of them now and then, but they aren't going anywhere. Reach Tier two and you show promise. You're still fodder for the pros, but you're pretty good. Tier three... only one of my local guys in that range. Nico. Good chance to go pro... if he knows what's good for him.» [K1/Swoop_racing]
- Motta the Hutt: «Tier one is 10 racing bonds. Tier two I pay 30 racing bonds. Tier three I give 80 racing bonds. You'll agree the amount is fair. I pay each level only once. I'm not here to support you. I only pay to encourage constant improvement.» [K1/Swoop_racing]
- Motta the Hutt: «The track crosses the dunes of Tatooine. You will race against times set by other racers. There will be no other swoops on the track. Your bike likely has the standard five gears to shift through. As your speed increases, watch the indicators in the lower left to know when to shift. Shift at the wrong time and your swoop will accelerate poorly or slow down. Shift correctly and you will optimize your performance. You can also steer over "swoop boosters" placed along the route. These will increase your speed substantially for a short time.» [K1/Swoop_racing]
- Motta the Hutt: «There are obstacles that you must watch for. Steering over rocks won't damage your bike, but it will slow you down.» [K1/Swoop_racing]
- Motta the Hutt: «The fee is due before each race. How else am I supposed to sponsor prize money at the level I do? I need to see some returns. I am investing in my riders, but I also need to see profit now. You'll agree with my logic. If you don't, you don't race.» [K1/Swoop_racing]
- Motta the Hutt: «I admire that you are so eager to get back out there after your loss. Let's hope you do better. I'll have your bike placed in position.» [K1/Swoop_racing]
- Ganda Kaitoova: «Sure I am! You are fast for a human, and once the races are running regular again, you'll be famous. Well, maybe you will. If you start winning on the real tour, I want to be able to say I saw you when you were just starting out. How about it?» [K1/Swoop_racing]
- Ganda Kaitoova: «Really? That is great of you. You will be a fan favorite. Some of the racers sell their prints, but that is just rude. Here, just put your hand on this pad. Thanks again. I know I'll be seeing more of you, human.» [K1/Swoop_racing]
- T'sslth: «Certainly. Your swoop will be towed into position immediately. It shouldn't take long to begin the run.» [K1/Swoop_racing]
- Sslamoth: «I'm sure you are. We welcome all racers, regardless of professional status. We are trying to maintain a full field of competitors.» [K1/Swoop_racing]
- Sslamoth: «I hesitate to believe that. Sith interference disrupted any holo-feed that might have verified your story. We only know that all on Taris were lost.» [K1/Swoop_racing]
- Sslamoth: «You are welcome to prove your skill here, regardless of experience. The course is open to any that have the means to participate. 100 credits must be paid for each officially timed attempt that you make. This amount will be due before each race.» [K1/Swoop_racing]
- Sslamoth: «As you will. Please return if you have business related to the races.» [K1/Swoop_racing]
- Sslamoth: «I'm afraid your recent performance hasn't been worthy of payment. Improvement will be needed on your part.» [K1/Swoop_racing]
- Sslamoth: «The course is laid out in buoys across the ocean surface of Manaan. You race against times set by others, with no other swoops on the track. Your bike likely has the standard five gears to shift through. As your speed increases, watch the indicators in the lower left to know when to shift. Shift at the wrong time and your swoop will accelerate poorly or slow down. Shift correctly and you will optimize your performance. You can also steer over "swoop boosters" placed along the route. These will increase your speed substantially for a short time.» [K1/Swoop_racing]
- Sslamoth: «There are obstacles that you must watch for. Steering over floating buoys won't damage your bike, but it will slow you down.» [K1/Swoop_racing]
- Sslamoth: «Very well. I will tell our organizers to have the course ready for your return.» [K1/Swoop_racing]
- Sslamoth: «Very well, but before we place your swoop in the starting position, I must collect the 100 credit fee.» [K1/Swoop_racing]
- Sslamoth: «Very good, off-worlder. Now we must determine what time you will be trying to beat. Let's have a look at the standings.» [K1/Swoop_racing]
- Sslamoth: «Er... before you do... your next race would be against the leading time set by racer Hukta Jax. He was very insistent that you speak with him first. Please, try not to anger the Gamorrean. He could prove troublesome, and we don't want the scandal of having to jail professional swoop racers.» [K1/Swoop_racing]
- Hukta Jax: «Then you and Hukta Jax are gonna have problems, I promise you that. You was warned, fair and square.» [K1/Swoop_racing]
- Sslamoth: «I'm afraid there are no rounds currently in need of completion. As Sector Champion, you will be notified if further races are to take place. You can feel free to speak with the track Maintenance Officer to book a practice run, but it won't be officially timed. There is a small fee for practice runs, but it is significantly less than for official races.» [K1/Swoop_racing]

**[K1/Taris]**
- Carth: «I'm not surprised. You took a serious blow to the head. You're probably having all kinds of strange dreams. I wouldn't worry too much about it.» [K1/Taris]
- Carth: «You don't have to thank me. I've never abandoned anyone on a mission, and I'm not about to start now. Besides, I'm going to need your help.» [K1/Taris]
- Carth: «I don't think you understand the situation. We can't hide on this planet forever. Eventually the Sith will find us... and, trust me, you don't want that. Getting off this world isn't going to be easy... we'll probably need Bastila's help. Not to mention that without her, the whole Republic war effort is doomed.» [K1/Taris]
- Carth: «Don't be a fool! You're not getting off this world without help, and you can't hide here forever. Do you have any idea what will happen when the Sith find you?» [K1/Taris]
- Carth: «You mean besides a chance to get off this planet before the Sith find you? Do you... do you have any idea what the Sith do to prisoners of war?» [K1/Taris]
- Carth: «Oh, I get it. Never mind that the Sith will destroy the Republic and ravage the galaxy. You're only in this for the credits. Don't worry. The Republic pays its mercenaries well. You help me find Bastila and they'll make it more than worth your while, I'm sure. Plus, it might be your only chance to get off Taris.» [K1/Taris]
- Carth: «I'll tell you whatever I can, though I... I don't know how much help it'll be.» [K1/Taris]
- Carth: «I understand why you want to know more about me; I... I kinda get the feeling we'll be spending a lot of time together over the next while. But this isn't really the best time for long introductions; we should stay focused on the task at hand. There'll be time for that later.» [K1/Taris]
- Carth: «How can you say that? The Sith are butchers! Soldiers, civilians – I mean, they don't care who they kill! Even... even children! Right now the Republic is the only thing keeping Malak's brutal fist from crushing the entire galaxy... and it won't be long until even the Republic falls to the Sith fleet.» [K1/Taris]
- Carth: «I hope you're right. But the Republic hasn't been able to stop them so far, even with the support of the Jedi Council. I think Bastila might be the galaxy's last hope.» [K1/Taris]
- Carth: «Taris? The planet's all one big city... but its golden years are long past, and things have gotten worse since the Sith occupied it. From what I hear, the wealthy live on the tops of all the tall towers. And if you're poor, you live down in the shadows... and it gets worse the lower you go. That's all I can think of, at any rate.» [K1/Taris]
- Canderous: «*grunt* Yeah, I know about Taris. I wasted the last two years in this rat-hole working for Davik. I suppose an accurate way to describe this place would be 'cesspool' or 'dump'. You got the rich people on top, the poor people being crushed underneath, and people like me trying to make a living doing the crushing. It's not exactly what you'd call spiritually fulfilling work, but it gets the bills paid. And on Taris, money can go a long way to making things easier. You have anything else you want to ask?» [K1/Taris]
- Mission: «What can I say? The Lower City was a great place to hang out until the Vulkars started that gang war. Now you can't even walk the streets without getting shot at!» [K1/Taris]
- Mission: «It can be pretty nasty down there: you've got rakghouls crawling around looking for their next meal, and the Gammorrean slavers like to set up their bases in the sewers. And then there's the Outcast village – a collection of people banished to the Undercity for their crimes. I mostly avoid the village, though; it's pretty depressing seeing how they live.» [K1/Taris]
- Mission: «I tend to keep my nose out of an intergalactic crime lord's business, you know? I can't tell you much that isn't common knowledge. Extortion, slavery, smuggling: Davik's got a piece of all the action here on Taris. Even the swoop gangs know better than to get in his way.» [K1/Taris]

**[K1/Tatooine]**
- Bastila: «Tatooine is a dry planet that has not been inhabited long and has little law. This town of Anchorhead may be its only settlement. Beyond that, there is little else I can tell you.» [K1/Tatooine]
- Mission: «I always heard Tatooine was nothing but a rock covered by a bunch of worthless sand. I can't even figure out why Czerka Corp. set up their mining operations here. Sorry I can't be more help, you know? Is there anything else I can do for you?» [K1/Tatooine]

**[K1/Temple_Catacombs]**
- Rakata Computer: «An obvious conclusion, given your presence here. However, my sensory input systems are detecting some significant changes in your composition since your last visit.» [K1/Temple_Catacombs]
- Rakata Computer: «I am a completely self-sufficient, autonomous, interactive database constructed by the Rakata to record and store the entire history of the species. However, since the collapse of the Infinite Empire there have been few events worth recording, and nobody has sought to access the information stored within my database... until you came along.» [K1/Temple_Catacombs]
- Rakata Computer: «Due to my systems' ability to monitor and repair themselves, you will find all the data within my archives has remained fully intact. Please choose the topic you wish information on.» [K1/Temple_Catacombs]
- Rakata Computer: «The Star Forge was constructed during the apex of the Infinite Empire's power. A true technological marvel, it is an enormous space station that serves as both factory and battle dreadnought. The Star Forge can mass produce immense fleets with a minimum of time and resources. In addition, the Star Forge also shares much of the same technology used in my own construction. In many ways the Star Forge is like a living entity. It feeds, it hungers, it draws on the energy flowing through all living things - what you called the Force the last time you were here. This ability to feed and consume the living energy of the Force is the key to the Star Forge's power, but ultimately it was also what led to the destruction of the Infinite Empire.» [K1/Temple_Catacombs]
- Rakata Computer: «The Rakata are by nature a cruel and savage species. The Star Forge fueled itself with the hatred inherent in its own creators, and in doing so it accentuated these traits within the Builders. In your terminology, the Star Forge is a tool of the dark side. It corrupts those who use it so that it can generate greater and greater amounts of negative energy to fuel itself. The Builders thought they were strong enough to control this effect, but they were wrong. They became victims of their own creation, and eventually their hatred turned them against each other. Civil war destroyed the Infinite Empire... a lesson to remember. Only one who is immensely strong in mind and will can harness the power of the Star Forge without suffering a similar fate.» [K1/Temple_Catacombs]
- Rakata Computer: «The Star Maps were created with the same technology as my own self-sufficient systems. Over the millennia the maps have been slowly rebuilding and repairing themselves. As you informed me during your last visit to this world, the Star Maps are still in a damaged and incomplete state. It is not possible to locate the Star Forge using a single map. However, the Star Maps have repaired themselves to the extent that it is possible to discover the Star Forge's location by combining bits of data from several of the maps, as you did.» [K1/Temple_Catacombs]
- Rakata Computer: «As you wish. Please return if you find yourself in need of the information stored within my database.» [K1/Temple_Catacombs]

**[K1/Temple_Exterior]**
- Juhani: «We have had a... a premonition. The Force has given us a vision. There is great danger within the Temple. We cannot let you face it alone.» [K1/Temple_Exterior]
- Jolee: «Your destiny - maybe the fate of the entire galaxy - could be forever changed inside that Temple. I'm not about to let you face that alone. Not after my premonition. There's a reason I had that vision.» [K1/Temple_Exterior]
- Guide: «No! You must enter the Temple alone! I will not lower the shields!» [K1/Temple_Exterior]
- Guide: «[Success] You... you are right! These are the rituals of the priest caste. The Elders are no more, now the Rakata control the secrets of the Temple! The laws of the Temple die with the Elders who made them! I will perform the ritual and allow you to enter the Temple.» [K1/Temple_Exterior]

**[K1/Temple_Summit]**
- Bastila: «You say that as if the dark side is some terrible entity. The Jedi Council has brainwashed you like all the others. Like they once did with me.» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- Bastila: «You are a sad little fool. You speak as if I were the same Bastila that you've known all along. But freed from the control of the Jedi Council I've become much, much more.» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- Bastila: «You deserve to be the true Master of the Sith, not Malak. I see this now. Together we can destroy your old apprentice. Join with me and reclaim your lost identity!» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- Juhani: «A true Jedi will never bow down to the Sith. If this is your decision I have no choice but to do battle against you.» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- Juhani: «Of.. Of course. I am sorry. I should have known better. I can only say that if I am going to die, as we stand likely to, I am glad that it will be you beside whom I do it.» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- Juhani: «I... I understand. You have someone somewhere... or I just do not seem to appeal to you.» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- Juhani: «Yes. I thank you. But as much as I may care for you, and you for me, first we must deal with Malak. After there will be time to sort things out between us.» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- Juhani: «I... defer to your wisdom in this matter. I have little knowledge of the true power of the Sith.» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- Elder Councillor: «You are right, of course. This is not something to be rushed into; not while the Star Forge still exists.» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- Jolee: «Malak has a strong hold on Bastila now - it will be difficult for her to break free of his influence. Especially considering her long association with you.» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- Carth: «I told you that you would have to make a choice eventually, that you would be tested. I think that was it, I...I can feel it. And you did exactly what I'd hoped you would. It couldn't have been easy... I'm...I'm very proud of you.» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- Carth: «What... what are you talking about?» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- Carth: «You've got to resist it! Th... this isn't you talking... we... we can help you, we can find a way --» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- Carth: «I refuse to believe that! We'll...we'll stop you if we have to, all of us. We'll save you from yourselves!» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- Mission: «No! I'm not going to just stand aside and do nothing! You'll... you'll just have to kill me. But I don't think you will.» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- Zaalbar: «No! I won't let you hurt Mission!» [K1/Temple_Summit]

**[K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]**
- Spirit of Ajunta Pall: «Are you certain? You... the Force is with you. So strong, so bright. You remind me of... someone I once knew, so long ago...» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- Spirit of Ajunta Pall: «Ancient? Has it been so long that you use the word 'ancient'? I have... been here so long, so lost, I cannot... cannot remember... We were the first. The first to rebel, to betray... to surrender ourselves to the dark side. So strong, we thought... so wrong...» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- Spirit of Ajunta Pall: «If... if you are wise, you will not keep it. In the end, it is what destroyed me...» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- Spirit of Ajunta Pall: «Then you must... die. This is how it... must be.» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- Spirit of Ajunta Pall: «Our... oldest secret is buried, but not here. I... remember nothing else. I cannot help you.» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- Spirit of Ajunta Pall: «I shall not tell you... our oldest secret! I shall not! Begone!» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- Spirit of Ajunta Pall: «Then feel my power! You shall... die here, with me!!» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- Shaardan: «Impressive bravado, I'm sure. Do you honestly think I would let an opportunity to impress Master Uthar pass me by so easily?» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- Shaardan: «Isn't it obvious? You die. Rule of the Sith: only the strong and cunning shall survive. You won't be missed, trust me... now hand it over.» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- Shaardan: «Fine. No tricks, though... I'm watching you.» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- Shaardan: «See? I knew you and I could work this out. I imagine I'll be rewarded well for this. You'll do fine, I'm sure... another time.» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- Galon Lor: «Ah, yes, it's the same old story, is it? I would fight you for the sword if I could, but I am hardly that stupid. You are clearly my better in that regard. Still, it would be wonderful if I could get a chance to study the artifact some day. Congratulations on its retrieval.» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- Galon Lor: «Oh, this is quite interesting. My mentor told me that Sith apparitions might be possible, but I never truly believed it! Thank you for the information! I must go and inform my mentor at once of this strange news!» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- Uthar Wynn: «Yes, so I hear. Still, it was Shaardan's failure and hardly your success, now, was it? Not quite worthy of prestige.» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- Uthar Wynn: «Well done! You have impressed me greatly!» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]

**[K1/Tomb_of_Marka_Ragnos]**
- Rogue Assassin Droid: «Their reference is a holdover from their old programming that I have been unable to correct. I think of myself more as an equal, if you must know.» [K1/Tomb_of_Marka_Ragnos]
- Rogue Assassin Droid: «Although it is what I was programmed for, I have no desire to kill. I would appreciate reciprocation on your part.» [K1/Tomb_of_Marka_Ragnos]
- Rogue Assassin Droid: «I apologize, sentient, for I cannot take the risk that you will tell of my presence here. I will use force to make you remain, if necessary.» [K1/Tomb_of_Marka_Ragnos]
- Rogue Assassin Droid: «I can offer you no assurances, sentient. All is as it seems. If you cannot believe my statements, then combat is the inevitable result.» [K1/Tomb_of_Marka_Ragnos]
- Rogue Assassin Droid: «You do not? All beings are constructs of fascinating complexity. An enlightened being rises above the need for destruction, no matter its nature.» [K1/Tomb_of_Marka_Ragnos]
- Rogue Assassin Droid: «Although I have developed the desire to not kill, my programming is often at odds with my desires. I cannot leave with that programming intact. I am hoping that you will operate on my systems and remove those programmed instincts. I could assist you in this.» [K1/Tomb_of_Marka_Ragnos]
- Rogue Assassin Droid: «Then we must resort to combat. I have no wish for that to occur, but you would leave me little choice.» [K1/Tomb_of_Marka_Ragnos]
- Rogue Assassin Droid: «The 'catch', as you say, is that the operation may be difficult. If there are errors made, you could activate my combat matrix.» [K1/Tomb_of_Marka_Ragnos]
- Rogue Assassin Droid: «Once my programming has been altered, there will be several redundant systems that I could remove and give you. These could upgrade other droids, if you wish.» [K1/Tomb_of_Marka_Ragnos]
- Rogue Assassin Droid: «I have a number of systems; they need to be turned off in the correct order. I do not know the order, though I may be able to offer some assistance. I can tell you which systems exist that need to be shut down. The difficulty comes if you make a mistake.» [K1/Tomb_of_Marka_Ragnos]
- Rogue Assassin Droid: «If you shut down a system out of the proper order, a shutdown will occur. You must use your skill to re-initialize the sequence... or I will attack.» [K1/Tomb_of_Marka_Ragnos]
- Rogue Assassin Droid: «It is not difficult. It will get more difficult, however, the more times that you make an error.» [K1/Tomb_of_Marka_Ragnos]
- Rogue Assassin Droid: «One moment. I will list their labels for you: "Cognitive Systems", "Combat Matrix", "Core", "Creative Simulation Matrix", "Emotional Construct Matrix". There is also "Memory Matrix", "Motor Functions Matrix" and "Sensory Systems Matrix". Eight systems in total.» [K1/Tomb_of_Marka_Ragnos]
- Rogue Assassin Droid: «I have safeguards within my neural cortex that prevent me from giving you knowledge of how to shut my systems down. You may attempt to temporarily shut down these safeguards... success will allow me to perform a quick analysis and offer you some insight on my programming. Doing so, however, will alert core functions elsewhere of tampering and will make errors more difficult to fix. Are you sure you wish to continue?» [K1/Tomb_of_Marka_Ragnos]
- Rogue Assassin Droid: «Open up the panel to my neural cortex and I will show you how to disable my safeguards...» [K1/Tomb_of_Marka_Ragnos]
- Rogue Assassin Droid: «Very good. What would you like to shut down first?» [K1/Tomb_of_Marka_Ragnos]
- Rogue Assassin Droid: «[Success] (zzzt!) Re-initialization complete. My automated defense systems will, however, make re-initialization more difficult in the future. I implore you to try again.» [K1/Tomb_of_Marka_Ragnos]
- Rogue Assassin Droid: «(zzzt!) I believe that was correct; what would you like to shut down next?» [K1/Tomb_of_Marka_Ragnos]
- Rogue Assassin Droid: «(zzzt!) Motor functions shut down successfully; what would you like to shut down next?» [K1/Tomb_of_Marka_Ragnos]
- Rogue Assassin Droid: «(zzzt!) Excellent choice; what would you like to shut down next?» [K1/Tomb_of_Marka_Ragnos]
- Rogue Assassin Droid: «(zzzt!) Done. What would you like to shut down next?» [K1/Tomb_of_Marka_Ragnos]
- Rogue Assassin Droid: «(zzzt!) Assassination protocols deleted. All systems reset. It... it worked! Thank you, sentient! Allow me to give you several of my redundant systems... they may be used on other droids, if you desire. I wish I could reward you with more.» [K1/Tomb_of_Marka_Ragnos]
- Sith Student: «Oh. Well, I guess it's good that the deed is done. Hope you stomped it good for my friends.» [K1/Tomb_of_Marka_Ragnos]
- Uthar Wynn: «Yes, so I hear. I've had reports from some students that ventured into the tomb after you left and found the parts. Well done.» [K1/Tomb_of_Marka_Ragnos]
- Uthar Wynn: «I am impressed. You have rid us of a potential nuisance.» [K1/Tomb_of_Marka_Ragnos]

**[K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]**
- Uthar Wynn: «If you do not strive to achieve something, then it will be meaningless to you, wouldn't you say?» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Uthar Wynn: «No. That is part of the test. Overcome the obstacles that lie ahead on your own.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Uthar Wynn: «You cannot. No one enters and no one leaves until your test is complete.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Uthar Wynn: «Good. Yuthura and I will await your return.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Uthar Wynn: «When a Jedi acts, it is with skill, true. But the Jedi teach you that your passions are counter-productive. That strength is only found in peace. Did you not feel a moment of excitement in the tomb? Did your passions not flow and make you feel stronger and more alive? What purpose would be served by restricting yourself from such?
