I would say only the purpose of the Jedi. Through this they would control you and restrict your potential.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Uthar Wynn: «Perhaps not, to someone of your natural strength. The point is in the recognition of the nature of the act rather than its sheer difficulty.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Uthar Wynn: «If you came to the academy, you must have felt the stirrings within you that cry out for the freedom of the dark side, that cry out for release. I can only show you the path that will lead to power and superiority. You have followed it this far... you must decide if you are capable of continuing.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Uthar Wynn: «This is where your path ends, young one. There is no turning back once you have embarked on it.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Uthar Wynn: «Good. The last part of your test will now commence. Here you will learn the lesson of competition. All life must compete in order to flourish. Such is the natural way of the universe. To stand still is to know death... one must always be moving forward. So is it the same amongst the Sith. Compete or die. Mercy is irrelevant. So it behooves you in this final test to strike down one you are familiar with, for no other reason than to prove you are superior and without mercy. Normally this would be against another student. You, however, get a special treat. You will fight Yuthura, here... my own apprentice.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Uthar Wynn: «You are indeed a fool if you think to oppose us both, young one.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Uthar Wynn: «You will never leave here!!» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Yuthura Ban: «D-damn you! You will never be a Sith, now! I... I will destroy you for this! You... you will not leave here alive!!» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Yuthura Ban: «D-damn you! I... I will destroy you for this! You... you will never leave here alive!!» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Yuthura Ban: «No, I don't suppose you can.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Yuthura Ban: «That's... tempting, but I don't think I could. Your path... it goes somewhere I can't follow.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Yuthura Ban: «[Success] I... suppose you could be right. All the things I wanted to do, all the wrongs I wanted to right... I haven't done any of it. They just get farther and farther from my mind. All I've cared about is power and... and myself. This isn't the person I was.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Yuthura Ban: «You're right. Maybe... maybe what I needed most was peace, after all. The Jedi tried to show me that. I don't know if I can ever go back to them... but my place isn't here. Thank you... thank you for showing me that.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Yuthura Ban: «I did. You told me I should rethink my path, and I did. You were right.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Yuthura Ban: «Yes, I suppose your fate still has much in store for you, just as I saw back on Korriban.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Yuthura Ban: «I don't know. I doubt it. Even if I wanted to, the Sith would never take me back. To them, I am a traitor. But the dark side... that's a different matter. The dark side is not the Sith. I still feel its temptation strongly. My emotions are so strong. Just the other day I nearly attacked a poor Padawan for tripping into me, nearly berated his clumsiness and weakness. I didn't, thankfully, but it's difficult to fight off those feelings. How do you do it?» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Yuthura Ban: «And to think I took my advice to come here from you. Ha ha! Perhaps you should follow your own counsel sometime.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Yuthura Ban: «You know, I'd forgotten all about that until I spoke to you about it on Korriban. Strange, considering my hate for them is what made me leave the Jedi. I think... I think I've got to help myself, first, before I can help anyone else. Maybe once that happens, I can work with the Jedi to make a difference. They may work slowly, but they do make a difference, don't they?» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Yuthura Ban: «That's... very tempting. But I'm not ready to leave here, yet. And I don't belong on the path you have to take... I told you that on Korriban. It's still true. But... perhaps you could come back, afterwards. I could certainly use a friend, then.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Yuthura Ban: «Then may the Force be with you, my friend. I hope we will see each other again.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Yuthura Ban: «Very well. I... won't stop you, or mention what you've done here.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Yuthura Ban: «As I thought. You are no Sith. A real Sith would have no hesitation in striking me down.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Yuthura Ban: «Then I hope you got what you came to the academy for, whatever that was.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Uthar Wynn: «So you did. And now I shall destroy you as I would any other Sith who betrayed me.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Uthar Wynn: «You... you fool! You will never... never be a Sith, now!!» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Uthar Wynn: «You have passed the final test, but I see it is not yet your destiny to join the order. Perhaps that will come in time, young one. There is much you must accomplish, first. The dark side has plans for you. As I said... return once that is done, if you desire.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Uthar Wynn: «You... I will destroy you, mongrel! I will rip the flesh from your bones!!» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Uthar Wynn: «May the Force serve you well, young one.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Computer Terminal: «You press the reset button. After a moment of flashing lights and whirring sounds, all the systems on the left pillar become active.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Computer Terminal: «You step back from the controls.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Computer Terminal: «To which pillar do you wish to transfer power for the Top System?» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow] *(+7 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Computer Terminal: «You transfer the power successfully...» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow] *(+14 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Computer Terminal: «To which pillar do you wish to transfer power for the Mid-Upper System?» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow] *(+3 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Computer Terminal: «To which pillar do you wish to transfer power for the Mid-Lower System?» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Computer Terminal: «To which pillar do you wish to transfer power for the Base System?» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Sith Apprentice: «What? You... but you aren't even a Sith, yet! Surely you jest!» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]

**[K1/Tomb_of_Tulak_Hord]**
- Jorak Uln: «No? Blast it! What have they been teaching you children in that academy these days!» [K1/Tomb_of_Tulak_Hord]
- Jorak Uln: «I'm sure we could. We could chat all day about it, but where would that get us? I'm quite determined, and you are quite helpless.» [K1/Tomb_of_Tulak_Hord]
- Jorak Uln: «Why, then you go free! Tell you what... I'll even pass onto you my own personal thesis on ancient Sith philosophies. They're based on all I've learned studying Tulak's tomb. It'll make you a better Sith, I'm sure. The chances of you passing, however, are sadly remote.» [K1/Tomb_of_Tulak_Hord]
- Jorak Uln: «Well, forgive me for resorting to the cliche, but you would die, of course.» [K1/Tomb_of_Tulak_Hord]
- Jorak Uln: «I don't remember offering you a choice. You can refuse to answer my questions, of course, but then I'm afraid I will just have to assume the worst.» [K1/Tomb_of_Tulak_Hord]
- Jorak Uln: «Then you win. If you die, then he wins. I thought this was self-evident. Did you not grasp the concept earlier?» [K1/Tomb_of_Tulak_Hord]
- Jorak Uln: «Incorrect! Do that and no-one is in command. You derived no benefit from the kill, and neither did the Sith. We are not savages, you know.» [K1/Tomb_of_Tulak_Hord]
- Jorak Uln: «So you've made a profit and now have some sniveling, useless fools trailing after you. No, no, no. Bad idea. Bad idea.» [K1/Tomb_of_Tulak_Hord]
- Jorak Uln: «Correct! Let them rip the secret from your cold, dead hands... if they are strong enough. Oh, certainly, you might share it with a few... selected... minions, but that of course is a completely different story. Ha ha!» [K1/Tomb_of_Tulak_Hord]
- Jorak Uln: «Correct! Publicly, if you can. There is no room for that level of failure. Not killing him would be seen as a sign of weakness... and then where would you be?» [K1/Tomb_of_Tulak_Hord]
- Jorak Uln: «What?! How did you know that? That was my trick question! Bah! Telepathy. No, wait! Someone told you didn't they!» [K1/Tomb_of_Tulak_Hord]
- Mekel: «Yeah, well... I see what you mean. I've never... I mean, I've never been on that side of the fence before. It makes you think.» [K1/Tomb_of_Tulak_Hord]
- Mekel: «Well... you can have those writings he mentioned. I won't fight you for them. Master Uthar will be very pleased with you if you give them to him, you know.» [K1/Tomb_of_Tulak_Hord]
- Mekel: «Ahh! I'll kill you! You won't get away with this!!» [K1/Tomb_of_Tulak_Hord]
- Uthar Wynn: «Maybe it's better to say that Mekel is dead, yes? Considering the circumstances, I am unimpressed... though you do now have one less opponent.» [K1/Tomb_of_Tulak_Hord]

**[K1/Undercity]**
- Outcast Beggar: «Have you no heart? Have you no compassion?» [K1/Undercity]
- Shaleena: «My name is Shaleena... you're from the up-world, aren't you? I've... I've never seen it. I was born here in the Undercity. Is it as nice as they say up there?» [K1/Undercity]
- Shaleena: «Not to you, I suppose. But you're probably used to its beauty by now.» [K1/Undercity]
- Shaleena: «Of course... I'm sorry. You're probably here for something important, right? You probably want to speak to Gendar. He's the leader of our village. But if you... if you have time later, maybe you could come back and speak to me again. I don't get much chance to talk with people from the surface.» [K1/Undercity]
- Shaleena: «It's... it's just a story to make little children smile. Rukil believes in it, though. Sometimes I can almost believe it myself, but then I look around and I see the ugly truth. *sigh* I guess we have to make the best of what we have though. If you really want to learn more about the promised land you should speak to Rukil.» [K1/Undercity]
- Shaleena: «You'd probably get more information from Gendar, the village leader. Or maybe Rukil. But I'll tell you whatever I can.» [K1/Undercity]
- Shaleena: «I don't know anything about that, but maybe Gendar could help you. Sorry... is there anything else you need?» [K1/Undercity]
- Shaleena: «Well, I was born here for starters. My parents were Outcasts, banished to the Undercity for some crime or other... they never really liked to talk about it. Everyone here is an Outcast... or the descendant of one. It's tough down here, but we survive. I guess it's easier for me, since I've never known anything different. Some of the people have given up all hope; they've become bitter and depressed. But most of us are good people... despite what our ancestors may have done. Gendar could probably tell you more. Or maybe Rukil. You could talk to them if you want.» [K1/Undercity]
- Shaleena: «Oh... okay. Well, if you ever need anything, or if you just feel like talking, come back and see me. I hardly ever get a chance to speak to someone from the up-world.» [K1/Undercity]
- Outcast Healer: «I'm only warning you for your own protection, up-worlder.» [K1/Undercity]
- Outcast Healer: «I can't help the infected ones. They have to die so that the rest of the village can live. I accept this. If you think that makes me a coward, so be it.» [K1/Undercity]
- Outcast Healer: «I can't stop you from going through the gates, up-worlder. But if the infected ones have already transformed into rakghouls you'll be walking into your grave.» [K1/Undercity]
- Infected Outcast: «You... you will help us? Bless you, up-worlder! But... you must hurry!» [K1/Undercity]
- Gendar: «You are generous to offer this to us, up-worlder... you have my thanks. But I know nothing of administering the serum. You should bring it to the village healer.» [K1/Undercity]
- Infected Outcast: «You... you have a cure? Please, give it to me. Please. Quickly, before it's too late.» [K1/Undercity]
- Infected Outcast: «We... we have nothing, up-worlder. We are Outcasts. All we have are the clothes on our backs. Please, up-worlder. Show us mercy. Give us the cure out of the goodness of your heart!» [K1/Undercity]
- Infected Outcast: «Quickly – I must inject it before it is too late. Yes... I feel it working! I feel the disease burning away! It... it is like a miracle! I am cured! Thank you, up-worlder. You have saved us from a fate worse than death. I only wish I had some reward to give you. Maybe you can find something worthwhile in the wreckage of the Republic escape pod.» [K1/Undercity]
- Infected Outcast: «Not long ago an escape pod crashed in the Undercity, far to the northeast of the village. We were going to try and salvage equipment from it but we were attacked by the rakghouls and infected. I'd tell you more if I could, but our salvage team never reached the pod. It's probably still there, unless some of the other up-worlders already found it and picked it clean.» [K1/Undercity]
- Gendar: «The oldest man in the village. He is a kind soul, but I am afraid he is growing weary of this world. He dreams of escaping, of finding a better world. If you wish to speak to him you can probably find him wandering about the village. As I said, a kind man – though he does cling to his illusions.» [K1/Undercity]
- Gendar: «Ask your questions, up-worlder. I will answer to the best of my knowledge, though I know little beyond the borders of the Undercity.» [K1/Undercity]
- Gendar: «There are two entrances into the sewers from the Undercity: one to the north-east of our village, the other to the south-east. But the sewers are dangerous, UpWorlder. If you dare to travel those dark tunnels you would be wise to go heavily armed, unless you wish to become a meal for the rakghouls and other foul creatures.» [K1/Undercity]
- Gendar: «As you wish, up-worlder. Should you have need of anything else, come speak to me. I represent the entire village and I will do my best to help you however I can.» [K1/Undercity]
- Rukil: «A question. You are uncertain. Bewildered. Perplexed. Understandable, I suppose. Even after a hundred years of life I myself still become confused at times.» [K1/Undercity]
- Rukil: «No, no, no. Not yet. I must be wary. Careful. You may be a false savior. Dare I share my secrets with you? No... not until you prove yourself.» [K1/Undercity]
- Rukil: «My apprentice is... lost. I sent her out into the Undercity to find... well, I cannot tell you. Not yet. Sadly, my apprentice has not returned. Please, up-worlder – will you help an old man? Will you seek out my apprentice in the Undercity? Her name is Malya. I must know of her fate, whatever it may be. I must know what she... found.» [K1/Undercity]
- Rukil: «Finding her may be difficult; Malya could be anywhere in the Undercity... but if you find her I will know you to be our true savior! Only then can I reveal my secret knowledge to you.» [K1/Undercity]
- Rukil: «Then... perhaps you are not the one I have been waiting for. Should you change your mind, up-worlder, speak with me again. Come to me if you learn the fate of my apprentice.» [K1/Undercity]
- Rukil: «You seek my wisdom, but I dare not share my knowledge with you. Not yet. Not until you prove yourself to be the one foretold, the one who will guide us to salvation. Find my apprentice. Discover what fate has befallen her and I will know you are worthy, and not another false prophet sent to mislead my people!» [K1/Undercity]
- Rukil: «It is as I feared, then. She joins the list of those who have given their lives in the service of our cause. But though I am saddened by this news, there is yet hope. By finding my apprentice you have proved yourself worthy, up-worlder. You are to be the beacon on our path to salvation. You will guide us to the Promised Land!» [K1/Undercity]
- Rukil: «You are marked, up-worlder – even my dim old eyes can see the mantle of destiny that cloaks you. Perhaps old Rukil knows you better than you know yourself. I am old – I have lived a hundred years in the Undercity, cast down into the darkness. I know the legends and history of our people – and now you must learn it, too.» [K1/Undercity]
- Rukil: «I offer you wisdom and you turn me away? Perhaps you are not ready to accept the role you must play in the destiny and salvation of my people. Very well, up-worlder. Return to me when you are ready to learn what you must learn.» [K1/Undercity] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Rukil: «You return, up-worlder. Are you ready now to learn what you must learn? Are you ready to learn of the Promised Land?» [K1/Undercity]
- Rukil: «I have nothing to offer if you help me in this. I hope the knowledge that you are helping the entire village escape a life of darkness and suffering is reward enough.» [K1/Undercity]
- Rukil: «It is many decades since my father set out on his journey, but I remember he was eager to explore the sewers. You might want to begin your search there. I wish you luck in your quest, up-worlder. The future survival of this village – of my people – depends on you.» [K1/Undercity]
- Igear: «Oh, don't worry, I'll make this quick. I've got an offer that might be worth your while.» [K1/Undercity]
- Igear: «Don't be a fool. I'm the only one here who can pay you for those journals. Rukil has nothing – he won't be able to come up with any kind of reward.» [K1/Undercity]
- Rukil: «Yes, up-worlder... well done. However, I see that this journal alone does not have enough clues for me to solve this mystery. Too many pieces are still missing. I'm afraid that only with all three journals – my grandfather's, my father's and my apprentice's – will I be able to discover the location of the Promised Land. I will leave this journal with you for safe keeping for now. Perhaps it will aid you in your search for the others. I wish you luck, up-worlder – for the sake of the entire village.» [K1/Undercity]
- Igear: «Fine – go to Rukil! But he won't be able to pay you anything for those journals. Come back and see me when you're ready to stop acting crazy and you want to cut a deal.» [K1/Undercity]
- Igear: «Right – your reward. Here's 100 credits; that's a pretty hefty payment for some old notes and maps, eh?» [K1/Undercity]
- Igear: «Excellent – now Rukil will never lead these people to the Promised Land! Now, is there something else you need? You want to look at my inventory?» [K1/Undercity]
- Igear: «Oh, you better ask Gendar – he's the spokesman for the village. I just run the store. I don't want  to make him mad by answering any questions. Gendar already doesn't like me.» [K1/Undercity]
- Igear: «Hey, you don't know what it's like down here! I have to claw and scrape to survive – nobody else will look out for me. But Gendar doesn't agree with my philosophy.» [K1/Undercity]
- Igear: «Yeah, okay. You come back and see me if you want to deal.» [K1/Undercity]
- Hester: «You monster! How can you say that? Somebody do something!» [K1/Undercity]
- Gate Guard: «Heh. I just guard the gates. Gendar is the leader of the village, up-worlder. You should speak to him if you have any questions.» [K1/Undercity]
- Gate Guard: «I will open the gate for you, up-worlder, but you've got to be quick. In a few seconds I must close and lock it again for the safety of the village.» [K1/Undercity]
- Gate Guard: «You would risk your life for a stranger? Heh. You are brave, up-worlder. I will open the gate for you, but you've got to be quick. In a few seconds I must close and lock it again.» [K1/Undercity]
- Mission: «Gadon? What? I... I don't know what you're talking about. Please – Zaalbar's in trouble. He needs help or they're going to sell him into slavery!» [K1/Undercity]
- Mission: «Listen, my friend Zaalbar is in serious trouble, so you can forget about me helping with anything else until I get him back!» [K1/Undercity]
- Mission: «Me... me and Zaalbar were just wandering around here in the Undercity. You know, looking for stuff we could find, just kind of exploring. We do it all the time.» [K1/Undercity]
- Mission: «No way. I'm the one that got Zaalbar into this mess, so I'm coming with you. I'm not going to abandon him again!» [K1/Undercity]
- Mission: «How can you be so stubborn? When you're ready to help me with Zaalbar come on back – because you'll never get into that Vulkar base without my help!» [K1/Undercity]
- Mission: «It's a deal – as soon as we get Big Z back, I'll show you a way into that Vulkar base! Now, come on – We have to find Zaalbar before they sell him to slavers – or worse!» [K1/Undercity]
- Canderous: «Mandalorians don't make threats, we make promises. But I'm just trying to give you a friendly warning.» [K1/Undercity]
- Canderous: «What are you, an off-worlder? Everybody knows Davik. He's a member of the Exchange. You know – the interstellar crime syndicate. Smuggling, gambling, extortion – Davik controls it all here on Taris. That's why I'm working for him. But lately the Lower City gangs have been giving my boss some trouble.» [K1/Undercity]
- Canderous: «This isn't really the place to have these kind of conversations. But I guess I could spare a couple of seconds for you – after all, you did help us out with those rakghouls. The Lower City is overrun with gangs, but the two most important ones are the Hidden Beks and the Black Vulkars. Used to be the Beks were the big dog and kept the others in line. But lately the Black Vulkars are moving in on Bek turf and challenging them for control. The Vulkars are even starting to defy Davik! Take these escape pods, for example. Everyone knew Davik would want first crack at them, but I know for a fact those damn Vulkars have already sent their own salvage party down here.» [K1/Undercity]
- Canderous: «Normally I'd make you regret those words, but I'm not wasting another minute down here. I'm responsible for this whole company, and they won't survive down here much longer.» [K1/Undercity]
- Republic Soldier: «Th... thank you.Those things – they attacked as soon as we came out the doors. They... they killed the others. It was... it was horrible.» [K1/Undercity]
- Republic Soldier: «Bastila? She wasn't in this pod. We were tracking her pod just before we hit the planet's surface. It went down somewhere to the west. We lost the signal when our own pod crashed. Maybe... wait – what was that? Did you hear that? I thought I heard something.» [K1/Undercity]
- Republic Soldier: «Over there... I heard something. I know I did! There it is again! It's those monsters! They're coming back! Run for your lives!» [K1/Undercity]
- Sith Patrol Leader: «Security papers? Oh... you're one of those trackers the Commander sent down, right? They should have given you an armed escort – it's nasty down here! We've already lost one patrol... we figure the rakghouls got them. We've had so many encounters with those things we've actually run out of rakghoul serum. Plus we've had several skirmishes with looters from those Lower City swoop gangs. I'm telling you, we should just stay in the Upper City where we're in control!» [K1/Undercity]
- Sith Patrol Leader: «Oh, is that why you're down here? Search and rescue? I didn't think they would send anyone. They were in the southern section of the Undercity when we lost contact. I would have gone to investigate, but my orders are to search for those crashed escape pods... even if it means leaving another patrol to die.» [K1/Undercity]
- Sith Patrol Leader: «Hey, I'd never disobey my orders... I'm not a fan of court martials and executions. I was just talking, you know? Nothing anyone has to mention to the Commander.» [K1/Undercity]
- Sith Patrol Leader: «You're questioning the Sith? How many people do we have to kill before the rest of you learn some respect? Well, I guess it's time for another lesson. Patrol... attack!» [K1/Undercity]

**[K1/Upper_City_Cantina]**
- Christya: «Well, I'm used to the finer things in life. I'm not about to lower my standards for any man. So how about it – you want to get those drinks for us?» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Christya: «I love a man who's not afraid to spend his credits! I'll meet you in the tap room. Don't be too long – I'm not a patient girl.» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Niklos: «My name is Niklos. I'm the unofficial champion of the Pazaak circuit here on Taris. If you don't mind losing your wager we could play a few hands.» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Niklos: «Questions? Heh. I don't come here to answer questions. If you want to play Pazaak, let's play. If not, then quit crowding my gaming table.» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Niklos: «You don't even have your own deck? Then why are you bothering me? You can't play Pazaak without your own deck! If you're serious about Pazaak, go speak to old Garouk on the other side of the tavern. His gambling days are over, and he's looking to sell his deck.» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Niklos: «You still don't have a deck? Then why are you bothering me? Until you get a deck from Garouk we have nothing to discuss.» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Garouk: «Hello there, youngster. You interested in buying the Pazaak deck of an old man looking to get out of the gambling game? Just fifty credits and I'll sell you all my cards. I'll even throw in a free lesson to boot. It's a great deal, if you can afford it.» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Garouk: «Don't get me wrong, the game's been good to me over the years. I've traveled the span of the galaxy, from the Core Worlds to the farthest reaches of the Outer Rim. I've won countless fortunes... and lost countless more. But that was long ago, and things have changed. Gambling is a young man's game. That's why I'm looking to get out. My offer still stands – fifty credits gets you my old Pazaak deck and a free lesson. A great offer if you can afford it.» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Garouk: «I'm sorry to hear that, but fifty credits is my best offer. I'm practically giving the deck away at that price. If you want it, it's yours. If not, no hard feelings.» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Garouk: «Glad to see you're interested in the grand old game. The rules are pretty simple. [[../Pazaak#Pazaak Rules» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Garouk: «Back again? Is there something old Garouk can do for you, or did you just come to chat with a lonely old man?» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Garouk: «Mmm. Don't know what I can tell you that would be any use. The Upper City is nice enough, though it was better before the Sith showed up. But they don't bother an old man like me much. Don't know much about the Lower City. It's rough down beneath. I just stay up here where it's safe and mind my own business.» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Garouk: «Of course, of course. No sense in a youngster like you wasting your days away with an old man in a dingy bar. But if you ever want to chat, you know where to find me.» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Bendak Starkiller: «What, you don't recognize me? I'm Bendak Starkiller, the most famous blaster-slinger on Taris. Over 100 duels without a loss – every one of them a death match!» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Bendak Starkiller: «Since I'm not going to answer them, you'd just be wasting your breath. So move along before people see us talking and think I'm signing autoprints today. I don't want to be mobbed.» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Bendak Starkiller: «Off with you then. I'm done with this conversation anyway.» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Bendak Starkiller: «So you're the one doing Zax's dirty work. Well I've got some bad news for you, little bounty hunter. There's no way you're bringing me in. This cantina is wired with a top-level security grid. You pull a weapon on me and the auto-fire turrets will fry you before you can even pull the trigger.» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Bendak Starkiller: «Why would I ever want to leave here? I've got food and drinks, a place to stay – and everybody treats me with the respect I've earned for my dueling career. But if you're set on collecting that bounty I'll make you a deal. I kind of miss the fight game; my hand is itching for the feel of a blaster in my grip.» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Bendak Starkiller: «Sorry, I don't waste my time with no-names. You have to prove yourself before you get a shot at me. I'm the real deal, kid – 100 death matches without a loss! But if you can knock off all these other pretenders in the duel ring, I might consider coming out of retirement to face you in one last death match.» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Bendak Starkiller: «Good luck with that, kid... you're going to need it. HA-HA-HA!» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Bendak Starkiller: «Hmmm... never figured you for a coward. You ever get the guts to fight me come on back and we can set the match up. Otherwise we've got nothing to talk about.» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Sith: «Good. Move along and leave me alone or I might have to run you in for questioning.» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Sith: «I don't wear my uniform when I'm off duty. It's not allowed. In fact, anyone in uniform is banned from entering the cantina. The officers don't like it when we show up here off duty. Don't like us fraternizing with the locals, I guess. But it gets pretty stale hanging out around the base all the time. Besides, the Sith don't own me. Being a soldier in their fleet is just a job, you know? A job with long hours and low pay, I might add.» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Sith: «I'm surprised to see you again. I thought you had gotten sick of me complaining about my job at the Sith military base. I guess I kind of obsess about it. That can't be healthy.» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Sith: «What? Are you offering me a bribe? You realize this is treason, right? If I report you the Sith will have you executed!» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Sith: «You... you weren't serious? Oh... okay. Although if you *were* serious, we might be able to work something out. No - wait! Forget I said that! We shouldn't be talking like this. I might complain a lot, but it's a big step from complaining to treason. I think maybe we should end this conversation - for both our sakes.» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Sith: «Hey, now - let's not start making threats. I didn't say I *would* report you, only that I could. This kind of talk is dangerous, you know? But I'm not going to turn you in.» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Sith: «Sorry to hear that, but I won't put my neck on the line for nothing! Tell you what - you come up with the credits and we can talk. Otherwise, no deal.» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Sith: «Okay, you've got a deal. The trick is to get you the uniforms without the Sith knowing I gave them to you. But I've got a plan. I'm going to a party with some of the other soldiers tonight. Most people are coming right after their shift, so they'll have their uniforms with them. You show up and I'll make sure everyone else gets good and drunk. When we pass out you can take our uniforms from the lockbox in the apartment.» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Sith: «We're in this together now. If either one of us goes to the Sith we'll both end up in the torture chambers. Anyway, it's not like you have a choice. I'm not giving you your credits back, so you might as well show up at the party later and take the uniforms after we all get drunk and pass out. What could be more simple?» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Sith: «I'll probably get a reprimand for not locking it up, but that's about it. Then, in another month I'll resign and retire with all those credits you gave me. Here - I'll add the address of the party to your datapad. Show up there later. To be on the safe side we shouldn't speak again before or after the party. I'll go make all the arrangements and I'll see you at the party tonight. Just remember - if you get caught with those uniforms, I don't know you.» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Sith: «Hey, there. I didn't think we'd be chatting again so soon. You took off on me pretty quick last time.» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Sith: «I'm actually a little surprised you're talking to me at all... most of the people here on Taris can't stand us Sith. It can make this job pretty lonely.» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Sith: «Don't try to analyze me. I'm just doing my job – same as any other soldier in any other army. Besides, I don't have to explain myself to you! I don't think we have much else to say to each other. Let's keep this civil and go our separate ways, okay?» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Sith: «Ah, it's okay. I might have overreacted a bit. But can you blame me? Everywhere I look I see one of you Tarisians glaring at me with hate in your eyes.» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Sith: «You know, it's like everyone on this backwater planet is in a permanent bad mood. Don't they know we have to make the best of things?» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Sith: «That's true. It's nice to meet someone who understands what I'm going through. It's good to talk about this stuff – It gets pretty lonely up at the military base. I have to get going soon – I've got a shift at the military base. But some of us junior Sith officers are having a party tonight to blow off some steam. I'd really like to see you again. Why don't you drop by the party? I'll show you where it is on your map.» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Sith: «Just because I'm with the Sith doesn't mean I don't know how to have a good time. You'll enjoy yourself. I promise.» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Deadeye Duncan: «I'm Deadeye Duncan, the number five ranked duelist in this cantina. You must have seen me in the dueling chambers, right?» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Deadeye Duncan: «You know – the dueling chambers: two people in combat, loser gets carried out unconscious on a stretcher. It's the only reason people come into this part of the cantina. Look, I'm the number five ranked duelist around here; I don't have time to explain all this. Go talk to the Duel Organizer if you're interested. He’s the Hutt over there in the corner.» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Gerlon Two-Fingers: «What? Why are you here if you don't even know what the dueling ring is?» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Ice: «You don't even know about the dueling ring? This is unbelievable! Then why are you even here? Wait – never mind. I don't really care.» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Ice: «Now you know why they call me Ice. If you're looking for a match, go talk to the Ajuur. He's that big Hutt over there in the corner. Otherwise, just go away.» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Marl: «No, we've never met. It's just that most people who come here to watch the duels are bored nobles looking for a little excitement in their drab lives. But you're different. You look like you know a thing or two about combat. I'm guessing you didn't come here to sit on the sidelines and watch a duel. Are you looking for a match?» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Marl: «My area of expertise is pretty much limited to the dueling ring and the other combatants here at the cantina, but I'll try to answer any questions you might have.» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Marl: «I can only tell you what is recorded in the basic visitor guides: the Upper City is a beautiful destination where intergalactic travelers can find all their basic needs. If you're looking for excitement the duel rings here are your best bet. Stay out of the Lower City – it's nothing but a gang ridden slum. Is there anything else you want to know?» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Marl: «You've come to the right man. I've been dueling here for nearly twenty years and I remember every duelist who's been through this cantina. Of course, there are only five of us left now: Duncan, Gerlon, Ice, Twitch and me. Bendak Starkiller comes in once in a while, but he's retired now.» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Twitch: «Ready Aim Fire!! Is your trigger finger as itchy as mine?» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Ajuur: «Questions? Bah – questions don't make me money! But if you want to fight, it could be very profitable for both of us. You interested?» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Ajuur: «Don't pass this opportunity up, human. You fight for me and I'll make you rich. If you change your mind you come back and talk to me again.» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Ajuur: «I can arrange for you to fight in the duel ring. If you win, you get the standard contract: 10% of all wagers. And don't worry, nobody dies in the duel ring anymore. Death matches are illegal now. Most fighters in the duel ring use vibroblades or stun sticks because the ring's pretty small, but a few of the duelists use blasters. Use whichever you prefer.» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Jergan: «What do you think of our local music? The band is quite good, wouldn't you agree? They're on the verge of intergalactic stardom, you know.» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Jergan: «Obviously you have an ear for music. Mark my words, they'll be famous soon enough. They were about to go on tour before this Sith quarantine stranded them here.» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Jergan: «Are you certain? This is a once in a lifetime opportunity – meet the legends before they were famous. All it will cost you is a small handful of credits.» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Jergan: «Well, I would like to help you but I can't arrange the meeting for free. And I'm afraid your feminine charms would have little effect on the Rodian bodyguards backstage. If you manage to come up with the credits just come back and see me. I'll be more than happy to arrange for you to meet the band. Until then, please enjoy the music.» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Jergan: «Excellent. Wait here while I go set it up with my contact. I'll give him your credits, then I'll come back out and take you to meet the band.» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Angry Woman: «You fell for his story, too? Well, I got some bad news for you sister – he ain't coming back. I gave him 20 credits last week, and he just disappeared with it!» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Carth: «*whisper* Careful what you say. I get the feeling this spoiled brat could cause us more trouble than she's worth.» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]
- Gana Lavin: «How dare you speak to me like that! Daddy's going to hear about this!» [K1/Upper_City_Cantina]

**[K1/Upper_City_North]**
- Taunting Boy: «Yeah – why would a human want to help you, freak?» [K1/Upper_City_North] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Taunting Girl: «Why do you care what happens to some scummy alien? He's just a freak!» [K1/Upper_City_North]
- Bastila: «Well I will. You children get away... just because he is different does not give you the right to attack him!» [K1/Upper_City_North]
- Ithorian: «You are too kind, human. I thank you for your generosity. Most of your kind here on Taris care little for aliens like myself.» [K1/Upper_City_North]
- Janice Nall: «What don't they do? They're great at making vehicle repairs, for starters. Pretty handy to have in a fight – small, but tough with a surprising amount of firepower. And there's no better code breaker or security system slicer around... not that I'd ever condone you doing anything illegal, of course.» [K1/Upper_City_North]
- Janice Nall: «Back again, I see. Well, I've never turned a potential customer away yet. You interested in seeing what I have in stock?» [K1/Upper_City_North]
- Janice Nall: «You want to see my inventory, or are you here to buy a utility droid? My prices are very reasonable, and all my droids come with a money back guarantee.» [K1/Upper_City_North]
- Janice Nall: «Of course. Right away, your Sithness.» [K1/Upper_City_North]
- Janice Nall: «I don't know if I can help you with your questions – I don't get out much. I prefer spending time with my droids to mingling with the Taris citizens. But I'll try to help you out.» [K1/Upper_City_North]
- Janice Nall: «Well, I don't know exactly what to tell you. Stick to the Upper City and you should be okay, as long as you don't do anything foolish to draw the attention of the Sith. Some of the Upper City citizens are biased against aliens – makes it tough for me to run my store – but a human like you shouldn't run into any problems. I wouldn't recommend going down into the Lower City unless you have business with Davik or something like that. The place is overrun with swoop gangs.» [K1/Upper_City_North]
- Janice Nall: «Davik Kang's a dangerous man. If it's illegal on Taris, he's got a hand in it. And I heard he's a member of the Exchange – you know, the intergalactic crime syndicate. I'd stay out of Davik's business if I were you. If you cross Davik you'll learn that his men are even more dangerous than the swoop gangs.» [K1/Upper_City_North]
- Janice Nall: «Maybe I'll see you around later. Don't forget my store motto: if you need droid parts, Janice Nall is your gal!» [K1/Upper_City_North]
- Janice Nall: «You know a bargain when you see one. This little guy's as reliable as used droids get. I did the reconditioning on him myself! Don't worry... he'll follow you everywhere! Is there anything else you need?» [K1/Upper_City_North]
- Janice Nall: «Glad to hear you're being reasonable. You have to understand that I'm trying to run a business here. Now, is there anything else you need?» [K1/Upper_City_North]
- Janice Nall: «Glad to hear it. I can't afford to make my customers unhappy... business is tough enough as it is. So, anything else you need?» [K1/Upper_City_North]
- Zaalbar: «No more filth than you're spreading, crazy man.» [K1/Upper_City_North]
- Gorton Colu: «Yes, my fellow human! You have seen the light! Gorton Colu has marked your face – I will remember you when the time to destroy the alien-lovers is upon us! For now, stay vigilant. Be ready. Trust no one. I will find you when the day of reckoning is upon us, and together we will rid beautiful Taris of these alien species!» [K1/Upper_City_North]
- Gorton Colu: «Greetings again, friend. Have you mentioned my Anti-Alien League to others? We must always be looking for those sympathetic to our cause.» [K1/Upper_City_North]
- Sith Guard: «If you were supposed to have them you'd know where to get them. Now quit wasting my time and move along.» [K1/Upper_City_North]
- Sith Guard: «I'm not here to give you information! I work for the Sith, not the Taris tourist board! Now just move along, unless you have the proper authorization papers.» [K1/Upper_City_North]
- Sith Guard: «Look, you've already shown me your authorization so you can go on down to the Lower City anytime you want. You don't have to keep bothering me. Just be careful down there. Those swoop gangs will take a shot at anybody. Even our Sith patrols have come under fire!» [K1/Upper_City_North]

**[K1/Upper_City_South]**
- Protocol Droid: «The Taris Tourism Board has issued a strong warning to all visitors to avoid the Lower City. It is a violent and dangerous place you would do well to avoid.» [K1/Upper_City_South]
- Protocol Droid: «Then if you will excuse me, I must continue my appointed rounds.» [K1/Upper_City_South]
- Kebla Yurt: «Ah, just so you know, the prices on the items are final – no bargaining here. This isn't a swap meet, ok? I only deal in top-notch stuff.» [K1/Upper_City_South]
- Kebla Yurt: «Uh, heh. It's not smart to say bad things about the people in power, if you get my meaning. I just wish the Sith would ease up on the quarantine – they're killing my business! I can't say I like having them here in the Upper City, but it could be worse. And we're still a lot better off than the Lower City, what with those swoop gangs and all.» [K1/Upper_City_South]
- Kebla Yurt: «Oh, you're one of those off-worlders, aren't you? Come to Taris for a short business trip and end up stuck here because of the Sith quarantine, right? Oh, you can't be too happy, trapped on an unfamiliar world and all. But Taris isn't so bad, as long as you stay in the Upper City. Just try to avoid the Sith. And stay out of the Lower City. The swoop gangs are totally out of control. Even Davik's men are having trouble down there.» [K1/Upper_City_South]
- Kebla Yurt: «Oh, Davik's a legitimate businessman, if you get my drift: smuggling, slaving, extortion. Uh huh. They say he's a member of the Exchange – you know, the big intergalactic criminal organization.» [K1/Upper_City_South]
- Kebla Yurt: «Careful where you say that. Davik's got spies and agents everywhere. Besides, he's as much a part of Taris as the skyscrapers or the swoop gangs.» [K1/Upper_City_South]
- Kebla Yurt: «Good day to you then. Remember to come on back to my shop if you ever need any supplies.» [K1/Upper_City_South]
- Sith Trooper: «Then you shouldn't be in full uniform! If you're off duty go change your outfit. If not, get back to your patrol route!» [K1/Upper_City_South]
- Zelka Forn: «I am quite busy maintaining the facility, but I suppose I could spare a bit of time to answer some questions. What would you like to know?» [K1/Upper_City_South]
- Zelka Forn: «I don't know much, but I'll tell you what I can. Do you want to know about Upper Taris? Or are you more interested in Lower Taris and the Undercity?» [K1/Upper_City_South]
- Zelka Forn: «The Lower City has been overrun by swoop gangs and thugs. If you're going to travel down there you better go armed. If you don't get attacked by one of the swoop gangs, you'll probably end up beaten and robbed by one of Davik's thugs! Soon it will be as bad as the Undercity.» [K1/Upper_City_South]
- Zelka Forn: «Davik is the resident crime lord of Taris. He deals in stolen goods, he trades in slaves and contraband, and it would take a month to list all the people he's made "disappear". His slimy grasp even reaches here into Upper Taris. Most of the shops pay him protection money so he'll leave them alone. Yeah, the man's nothing but a thug! I guess I'm lucky. Since my medical facility doesn't make any profits Davik doesn't bother me. But if you ask me he's no better than the Sith!» [K1/Upper_City_South]
- Zelka Forn: «Welcome back. Are you in need of healing or medical supplies? I can treat you right here at the center for almost any condition, except the rakghoul disease, of course.» [K1/Upper_City_South]
- Zelka Forn: «It will just take me a moment while I examine your injuries, and then I'll administer the proper treatment.» [K1/Upper_City_South]
- Zelka Forn: «Of course. Just step over here and I'll show you what I have in stock. My prices are very reasonable, just what I need to keep this facility operating.» [K1/Upper_City_South]
- Zelka Forn: «I couldn't afford to give you more than a few medpacs and a handful of credits. The medical center can barely afford to stay open as it is. But it hardly matters, anyway.» [K1/Upper_City_South]
- Zelka Forn: «Please don't say that! If the Sith hear you they might think I'm suggesting you start attacking their patrols. They could shut me down! I only mentioned the serum because you asked. I don't actually expect anyone to get me the serum. Now, is there anything else I can do for you?» [K1/Upper_City_South]
- Gurney: «Zelka isn't the only one who wants to get his hands on the rakghoul serum. Davik Kang will pay you ten times what Zelka can if you can get the cure.» [K1/Upper_City_South]
- Gurney: «Davik isn't the kind of guy you can just walk up to, you know? He likes to keep his business at arm's length. The best thing to do is take the rakghoul serum to Zax. He runs the Lower City bounty office, but everybody knows he also works for Davik. He'll make it worth your while.» [K1/Upper_City_South]
- Gurney: «Look, Zelka can't afford to pay me much. If you sell the serum to Davik, I can probably get a nice finder's fee for directing you to him.» [K1/Upper_City_South]
- Gurney: «Hmph. I'll just deny it. Who's Zelka going to believe – me, or some off-world stranger? Now, be smart about this. You'll get a better deal selling to Davik.» [K1/Upper_City_South]
- Gurney: «Helping people is all well and good, but you have to help yourself first, right? I'm telling you Davik will pay big credits for the cure. More than Zelka could ever afford.» [K1/Upper_City_South]
- Zelka Forn: «You have the serum? Impossible! How did you get this? No, wait... I don't really want to know. Can... can I see it? The serum, I mean? I need to see if there is enough for me to analyze it so I can start producing it in mass quantities.» [K1/Upper_City_South]
- Zelka Forn: «Then I guess there's nothing I can do to stop you. I only hope you'll change your mind later on.» [K1/Upper_City_South]
- Zelka Forn: «You truly have a noble and generous spirit, but you deserve something for your effort. Tell you what, I'll give you a discount whenever you shop at my store. It's the least I can do.» [K1/Upper_City_South]
- Zelka Forn: «[Success] I suppose I could give you a discount here at my shop, in addition to the credits and medpacs. Other than that there is really nothing else I can offer.» [K1/Upper_City_South]
- Zelka Forn: «You're threatening me? I... I don't want this to become violent. Look, here's an extra fifty credits. That's all I have, I swear!» [K1/Upper_City_South]
- Zelka Forn: «N-nothing. This... this isn't your concern. Just... just forget you saw anything, okay?» [K1/Upper_City_South]
- Carth: «No, it's *not*. The Sith would torture these men, or worse. You're not threatening this man while I'm present.» [K1/Upper_City_South]
- Zelka Forn: «Fine, take these credits to seal your tongue, then leave this place. I don't want to have anything to do with a monster like you!» [K1/Upper_City_South]
- Zelka Forn: «Thank you for keeping my secret. I only wish there was more I could do, but medical science has only come so far.» [K1/Upper_City_South]
- Bounty Hunter: «Smart move to mind your own business.» [K1/Upper_City_South]
- Bounty Hunter: «Guess we have to teach you to mind your own business!» [K1/Upper_City_South]
- Bullied Merchant: «You already helped me by saving me from those bounty hunters. So unless you have a spare 100 credits to give me so I can pay off Davik, there's nothing else you can do.» [K1/Upper_City_South]
- Bullied Merchant: «You're giving me 100 credits? Just like that? I... I don't know what to say! Thank you! Thank you!» [K1/Upper_City_South]
- Carth: «Are you joking? We don't need this guy's credits. Just go on, we were happy to help.» [K1/Upper_City_South]

**[K1/Upper_Shadowlands]**
- Gorwooken: «I'll operate the basket, as Chuundar has commanded, but I won't answer your questions while I stand in the Shadowlands. This place is sacred ground. Many of my kind have proven themselves here, and many have died as well. You aren't worthy of this place. I won't speak to you while we're here.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Jolee: «I'm sure you are, but you have to take time to listen. Really, you shouldn't try so hard to make new enemies.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Jolee: «Don't coddle me, child. I'm neither a Jedi nor your master. I'm just an old man that's been lost in the woods for far too long.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Jolee: «Now why would you be asking about that, hmm? Don't answer. I knew that had to be why you were here.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Jolee: «What I'm saying is that there are literally walls in your way, and you won't find what you need without my help... and my help has a price.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Jolee: «The Czerka poachers. I can smell that scent they use on you. I don't care what you told them, I want them gone.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Jolee: «Oh, save it. I know this sounds absurd, but I'm old and entitled to work you around a bit. Besides, the test is simple.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Jolee: «You presumably have a ship. I've seen all I wish to here. Isn't that enough? I mean, I'm really sick of the trees. And perhaps your destiny might show me something new. You never know.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Jolee: «Don't talk back to your elders, child. Look, they know me quite well, and are always on alert when I approach. The only way I could get rid of them would be violence, which would eventually lead to trouble for the Wookiees. I want them gone, but with as little fuss as possible. They have already caused enough trouble as it is.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Jolee: «Good. Return to me here and we will see how you have done. Shoo. Shoo!» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Jolee: «They are not hard to find. Their camp is to the far northeast. Try to get them to leave without too much fuss. After you finish, I will make it so you can pass the barrier that prevents your access to the lower Shadowlands.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Jolee: «Ah, and it makes you think I may have the taint of the dark side in me, doesn't it? Ooh. Is that what you are wondering, huh? Ooh.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Jolee: «I told you my price. After you complete my task I'll make it so you can pass the barriers blocking your goal. That's all I will add on the subject. I saw them put it in, so only I know how to manipulate it. That knowledge will serve me now. Do as I ask.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Jolee: «Well hurry up! You don't want to be stuck here as long as I have.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Jolee: «Czerka Corporation was smart to put him in power. He's as good at destroying Wookiee culture as dropping corpses full of Ardroxian Flu. Will you work for him or against him, I wonder? I will be interested to see.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Jolee: «Uh, sure, how's this: think for yourself. I don't care for politics, and I'm big on letting people figure things out for themselves. The Wookiees have prophesies of deliverance if you ask them, but for now they follow without hesitation. That is their wish.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Jolee: «Maddened with grief, perhaps, but not crazed. I helped him pass to the lower forests where only a Wookiee could follow. Some other matters will determine if you can follow. There is a barrier that... well, we'll talk of that more in a moment.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Jolee: «It is a very unfortunate matter. It will end in shame and sorrow. You will find Rorworr down here. And more. I have witnessed a number of happenings, but I have left them for others to sort out.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Commander Dern: «That crazy old fool is still alive? I suppose he's still upset that we don't all live in little homemade shacks like he chooses. I've got a job to do here, and there's nothing you or that old coot can do about it.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Commander Dern: «He's got no authority with me. I'm with Czerka Corporation. He's just some pet we keep so the locals stay in line. As long as you don't get in my way, you can wander where you want. I've got a job to do.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Commander Dern: «If you made it this far you know a fair bit already. What would I be able to tell you?» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Commander Dern: «It's not worth it. You're fighting the plants as well as the animals. Kshyy vines are tough as metal, and grow back fast. Instead, head office installed repulsor fields to keep the big predators out. Permanent placements too. We'll be fine for years.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Commander Dern: «Is that old hermit Jolee trying to talk you into doing his dirty work? We've spent too much on setting up just to walk away. As long as the sonic emitters keep the preds away and I've got bodies to guard the perimeter, we're staying right here. I mean, if I can put up with my own worthless guards complaining about this place, Jolee whining through you is nothing.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Guard: «What do you want to know about that? It's just one of five or so sonic emitters protecting the camp.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Guard: «Don't you start with me! I don't know what you said to that other guy, but I'm not risking getting killed! Now leave me alone before the Commander gets any ideas.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Commander Dern: «I don't need this kind of garbage from you. Watch your mouth. I'm the only law there is down here. If you've got something to say, get on with it. Otherwise, leave me alone. I've credits to earn.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Commander Dern: «All right, I'm done talking with you. You've got under my skin one too many times! I'll bury you down here!» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Guard: «Are you threatening me? Unless you want a fight with all of us I'd suggest you change your tone. Besides, enough things down here want to kill me. I don't need to add you to the list.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Jolee: «You were here and it needed doing. Besides, dealing with the Czerka is dirty business. I hope you washed your hands.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Jolee: «Well, perhaps I have a duty to point out what you should have learned from this. Or perhaps... no.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Jolee: «I see. You are completely detached. Are you sure you're not a droid? It doesn't matter. A trivial thing like this is no measure of anyone.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Jolee: «Oh, go bark up a tree. You'll get the same response out of me. Besides, I'm not about to get in the habit of grading you.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Jolee: «A good teacher, and I'm not saying I am one, but a good teacher doesn't mash a book in a student's face shouting "learn this bit here!" If you can't look at the situation for yourself and see the logic behind my way of doing things, you're not the type to understand me anyway. Besides, you're a little old for that master/student fuss, aren't you? I know I am.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Jolee: «Yes, of course. Let's get moving. I noticed the old paths some time ago. You probably wouldn't have found them on your own.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Jolee: «You aren't going to ask about it? A repulsor field in the woods and you have no interest?» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Jolee: «I think it was an attempt to block the more dangerous carnivores from wandering freely, but it's not very effective. It limits us non-climbers, but the creatures of Kashyyyk are very adaptable. You'll see why when we reach your goal.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Commander Dern: «Hurry up, I'm wasting too much time on you as it is.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Commander Dern: «All right then, I'll set you up. First you need to be able to draw them out. It's easy. I'll spray you with this scent... it drives them crazy. They can't help but attack you, even if it's a hopeless fight. Then you just carve them up. There's a big fleshy bulb in their brains that you want to scoop out. You can't miss it. I'd feel guilty if the money weren't so good. Come back here when you've got the glands and I'll pay you.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Commander Dern: «I'll take whatever you have. Keep it up and we'll both be rich. At least until we bleed this planet dry, hahaha!» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Broken Droid: «[You back away. The droid appears to suffer some sort of additional interior trauma.]» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Grarwwaar: «I am no slave, human! I am Grarwwaar, a free Wookiee. I do not answer to you.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Grarwwaar: «So you have uncovered my little secret. No matter, this body has grown tiresome to me. Maybe I can find one more to your liking, human.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Tach: «[This tach may be Rulan Prolik in disguise, but you can't tell for sure. Maybe if you kill it, it will revert to it's true form... assuming it's Rulan.]» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]

**[K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]**
- Dak Vesser: «Back again? Something... oh. Juhani... is... that you?» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- Dak Vesser: «Look... I don't want to be here after you leave. There'll be too many questions. So ask what you have to ask and I'll be on my way.» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- Dak Vesser: «It's the second tomb on the left when you enter the valley. Naga Sadow was the leader of the old Sith Masters here, we think. Darth Malak found something in there a while back, however, and ever since then only Master Uthar can enter it.» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- Dak Vesser: «Alright. Just be careful if you head into any of those tombs.» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- Dak Vesser: «Why don't you ask her?» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- Dak Vesser: «She and I were... students together at Dantooine, for a time. We were close, and we shared doubts about the Jedi's ways. I left for the Sith because I thought it would be better here. It looks like Juhani changed her mind and stayed with the Jedi after all. Your doing, maybe? Anyway... I'm done with the Jedi, the Sith... and with her. Just leave me alone.» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- Dak Vesser: «I already said, didn't I? I don't want to still be around when they start asking questions about you.» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- Dak Vesser: «Why do you care? I've no intention of opposing you.» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- Dak Vesser: «Is there some reason you should stop me from going? Are you afraid I'm going to tell someone you're here? What are you going to do, kill me?» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- Dak Vesser: «I may have studied with the Sith, but I've no special loyalty to them. You can believe me or not, as you wish.» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- Juhani: «Leave him be! I said that he would not betray you. Just... let him go.» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- Galon Lor: «Galon Lor, at your service. Aren't these tombs fascinating? We had no idea the Sith were here so long ago.» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- Galon Lor: «Certainly. Finding such an artifact and giving it to Master Uthar would earn anyone big points. Although I suppose you could also keep it...» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- Galon Lor: «Of course. Don't let me keep you from your business.» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- Lashowe: «Good. You have my... appreciation... for all your help. Good luck in your efforts.» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- Lashowe: «You're the one that convinced me to go through with this plan. Why wouldn't I see it through?» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- Lashowe: «Forget it. There is no guarantee. You helped me, I'll help you. Or don't you trust me?» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- Lashowe: «Forget it. I'm not waiting up for you. That wasn't our plan, anyway.» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- Lashowe: «Back off, I'm warning you! I'm not giving this up!» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- Lashowe: «You're not getting the holocron. This was my idea! The plan was that I take it back, not you!» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- Uthar Wynn: «*chuckle* I had heard that such an artifact existed. Tell me, young one... did you acquire this device by yourself?» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- Uthar Wynn: «You lie. Still, what matters is the fact that you succeeded. And Lashowe... does she yet live?» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- Uthar Wynn: «You didn't kill her? How very foolish. Now you have an enemy to contend with. The holocron, itself, is an impressive item, however.» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- Uthar Wynn: «She did. Prestige is not shared, however, if that is what you are wondering. If you aided her, it was to her benefit and not yours.» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- Lashowe: «So? That's your problem, not mine.» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- Lashowe: «Ha ha ha! Look... either do something about it or run along, will you? I have prestige to earn.» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- HK-47: «Commentary: I like the sound of this! Warming up blasters... on your command, master!» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- Lashowe: «I thought not. Get lost, then, will you? I'm busy.» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- Lashowe: «Ha ha ha! Oh, I wonder what Master Uthar will think of this! Alright, then... you have yourself a fight!» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- Uthar Wynn: «Killed her out of revenge, did you? Well done. I can appreciate such drama, believe me. I am most impressed that you have eliminated an opponent so handily.» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- Sith Student: «If you say so. Just thought I would warn you, is all.» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- Sith Student: «Seems that way. They say that its audio receptors became too sensitive and all the noise drove the droid nuts. If you're planning on going in, I'd suggest you use some sound suppressors or something... or you'll probably become a target right away.» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]

**[K1/Viewing_Platform]**
- Darth Malak: «Of course. What else do you have? Fate and destiny have conspired to keep you alive despite my best efforts; they have thrust you into the role of savior.» [K1/Viewing_Platform]
- Carth: «The Republic fleet picked me up from the planet. I told them of your betrayal... but I also told them I couldn't abandon you. I had to come. I said I'd find a way to save you from yourself, and I will.» [K1/Viewing_Platform]
- Carth: «I don't believe that. I don't think you do, either.» [K1/Viewing_Platform]

**[K1/Village_of_Rwookrrorro]**
- Chuundar: «Did you think you could walk the upper boughs of Kashyyyk and not be observed? I've had climbers watching you, and your ship.» [K1/Village_of_Rwookrrorro]
- Zaalbar: «You work alongside the slavers. You are no better than they are!» [K1/Village_of_Rwookrrorro]
- Chuundar: «You may talk, but no one will believe you. I've had a long time to guide what my people think.» [K1/Village_of_Rwookrrorro]
- Chuundar: «There will be no fight. You wouldn't survive. The village believes in me. They would rise to kill you. I also have the protection of Czerka Corporation guards as well. No, you have no chance while I am unopposed.» [K1/Village_of_Rwookrrorro]
- Chuundar: «I haven't killed Zaalbar because he is my brother and I hoped he and I could come to an agreement. You are irrelevant. I may be able to put you to use on a menial task. Zaalbar will have to stay here, of course.» [K1/Village_of_Rwookrrorro]
- Chuundar: «It isn't your choice, {{s» [K1/Village_of_Rwookrrorro]
- Chuundar: «We only enter the Shadowlands for ritual hunting. I'd leave the troublemaker down there but he has proven too good at surviving. He has managed to linger a number of years, cowering in his madness and shame. You are expendable. You will remove him.» [K1/Village_of_Rwookrrorro]
- Chuundar: «I don't know anything about something like that, and I don't care. Your concerns are nothing to me.» [K1/Village_of_Rwookrrorro]
- Chuundar: «Zaalbar didn't tell you? He took offense to my trading a few of our people for favors and weapons. I didn't understand his objection. Imagine the destruction if Czerka Corporation had come in with blasters firing. I saved many lives. Of course, I also gained weapons, and arranged for certain rivals to be taken. I protected my allies, and my power grew.» [K1/Village_of_Rwookrrorro]
- Chuundar: «Each has come to understand the benefit of working with the enemy. Unlike your exiled companion.» [K1/Village_of_Rwookrrorro]
- Chuundar: «You test my patience. Do my task or flee to your ship. I don't care which.» [K1/Village_of_Rwookrrorro]
- Chuundar: «You don't have much choice. You can flee the planet if you want, but I am keeping Zaalbar here with me. He'll stay until the mad-claw is dead.» [K1/Village_of_Rwookrrorro]
- Chuundar: «What made you think I wanted this to be easy for you? Besides, you won't need him. My people are trained to understand you.» [K1/Village_of_Rwookrrorro]
- Chuundar: «Do you speak of Freyyr, our father? Why? He was the one that ordered you exiled when you attacked me.» [K1/Village_of_Rwookrrorro]
- Chuundar: «No more words, outsider. I've given my orders. I can't hold you here, but Zaalbar will stay until you kill the mad-claw in the Shadowlands.» [K1/Village_of_Rwookrrorro]
- Chuundar: «The guards will let you pass now. Leave the village through the northwest and follow the platform to the south and then east. You'll eventually see where Gorwooken waits for you. He'll lower you to the forest floor. Go.» [K1/Village_of_Rwookrrorro]
- Wookiee Guard: «You be allowed audience when you return after completing his task. Only then will you have reason.» [K1/Village_of_Rwookrrorro]
- Wookiee Guard: «The village exit is northwest of here. Once outside, follow the platform to the east until you get to where Gorwooken is waiting. He will lower you to the Shadowlands, though I would prefer that you were simply dropped. You'll die down there anyway.» [K1/Village_of_Rwookrrorro]
- Wookiee Guard: «If the forest holds any justice you will die down there, as would all outsiders.» [K1/Village_of_Rwookrrorro]
- Wookiee Guard: «Others may speak to you, {{s» [K1/Village_of_Rwookrrorro]
- Wookiee: «Why? So you can better exploit us? Why should I speak with you?» [K1/Village_of_Rwookrrorro]
- Wookiee: «I am ashamed that he would ask outsiders to track such a creature. Why couldn't our own hunters remove his dishonor? If you must be the one, then so be it. Be merciful and quick. A Wookiee without the honor of self-control is a mere animal. And when you are through, do the same to your slave Zaalbar. His taint is a curse upon us too. Bah, I will speak no more of him.» [K1/Village_of_Rwookrrorro]
- Wookiee: «I shall believe that when I see it. I will speak with you, but do not mistake that for trust.» [K1/Village_of_Rwookrrorro]
- Wookiee: «What right do you have to question Chuundar? What could you possibly know about us?» [K1/Village_of_Rwookrrorro]
- Wookiee: «Your interest is surprising. I had not thought an outsider would care. Still, I shouldn't talk of our troubles.» [K1/Village_of_Rwookrrorro]
- Wookiee: «I would not believe you. You are an outsider. You lie. You cheat. You enslave us. There is nothing you could say to make me believe.» [K1/Village_of_Rwookrrorro]
- Wookiee: «I don't know about anything like that, but I don't understand you body-bald fools. You are all slavers or crazy.» [K1/Village_of_Rwookrrorro]
- Wookiee: «Our hunters say one of you has been living in the Shadowlands, and that is crazy! I don't know how he survives. It's probably just hunter-tales, though. No one could live in the Shadowlands for the years he has. No one.» [K1/Village_of_Rwookrrorro]
- Wookiee: «What makes it your business? Bah, I have nothing to say. Speak with his friends, if you must. Woorwill and Jaarak maintain a vigil in Woorwill's home on the central platform on the village.» [K1/Village_of_Rwookrrorro]
- Wookiee: «Woorwill and Jaarak are his friends. You could speak with them. They are in Jaarak's home on the central village tree platform.» [K1/Village_of_Rwookrrorro]

**[K1/West_Central]**
- Jolan Aphett: «I suppose we could play a match just to help you get a feel for the game.» [K1/West_Central]
- Jolan Aphett: «Well, if you feel like it, that's what I'm here for.» [K1/West_Central]
- Jolan Aphett: «Well, if you get enough, come back and maybe we can have a game.» [K1/West_Central]
- Selkath Port Official: «Your dislike of us only seems matched by our dislike of you. Personal feelings do not factor into our legal system however. And ALL must obey our laws.» [K1/West_Central]
- Selkath Port Official: «The single most important law on Manaan is very simple. kolto smuggling is punishable by death. If you're carrying any unprocessed kolto, you better have a permit.» [K1/West_Central]
- Selkath Port Official: «Your opinion is biased by your own alliances, human. Only we Selkath can best protect the interests of Manaan and Ahto city.» [K1/West_Central]
- Selkath Port Official: «There is a docking fee of 100 credits. You will have to pay this fee each time you dock on Manaan, or you will not be permitted into Ahto city.» [K1/West_Central]
- Selkath Port Official: «Good. Now you can stop bothering me. I have work to do.» [K1/West_Central]
- Selkath Port Official: «Then you won't be passing the gates into Ahto city. Feel free to leave Manaan whenever you wish. Should you decide to pay the fee, speak to me again and I will give you access to Ahto City.» [K1/West_Central]
- Selkath Port Official: «The Manaan board of trade and tourism thanks you, off-worlder. Here is a visitor guide and map to the city. Refer to it should you have any questions.» [K1/West_Central]
- Selkath: «Good! Your intrusion has already wrought havoc upon my hectic schedule.» [K1/West_Central]
- Selkath: «I have far more pressing matters than any questions you might have, off-worlder. This kolto is the backbone of Manaan's economy.» [K1/West_Central]
- Selkath Coordinator: «I am not here to answer your questions, off-worlder. I am a distributor of kolto - the lifeblood of the Manaan economy. Either show me your purchase permit, or leave me in peace.» [K1/West_Central]
- Selkath Coordinator: «Then why are you here, human? Are you seeking employment? We do not hire your kind here. Do you even have an off-worlder work permit? I thought not.» [K1/West_Central]
- Selkath Coordinator: «One does not simply 'get' a purchase permit! Such permits are brokered between intergalactic governments and the Ahto City Export Authority. We do not sell unrefined kolto by the handful - we sell it by the ton! Go to a store if you wish to purchase kolto for personal use.» [K1/West_Central]
- Selkath Coordinator: «Good - I am far too busy to converse with the likes of you.» [K1/West_Central]

**[K1/Woorwill's_Home]**
- Jaarak: «I have nothing to say to you. Go and learn what you can while bleeding our planet of its people and honor.» [K1/Woorwill's_Home]
- Jaarak: «You are an outsider. Your kind have done more damage here than any one of you could ever hope to fix. You taint us... and bring out the worst in everything. You will get no respect from me.» [K1/Woorwill's_Home]
- Woorwill: «Then why are you here?» [K1/Woorwill's_Home]
- Woorwill: «Oh, please don't. I haven't been paying attention to anything sinc Rorworr went missing. Do you know anything about that? I hope not. I hope you slavers didn't get him. N-not that you're a slaver. I'm sorry. I'm very tired. I haven't slept much lately.» [K1/Woorwill's_Home]
- Woorwill: «Rorworr? Oh, he's the greatest! He's led hunting parties to the Shadowlands, and even fought outsiders! Slavers... like you probably are. He's taught me a lot. He said I could grow up as strong as him, that I'd fight you too. I will, too. I will.» [K1/Woorwill's_Home]
- Jaarak: «Stop bothering the boy, outsider! He doesn't need you taunting him.» [K1/Woorwill's_Home]
- Jaarak: «No he doesn't! You don't say another thing to him! You want to lie, you lie to me, outsider!» [K1/Woorwill's_Home]
- Jaarak: «What... what are you talking about? I warn you, outsider, I won't tolerate your lies in my home.» [K1/Woorwill's_Home]
- Jaarak: «No! Don't say anything else! I can't let you... I killed Rorworr. Tell Worrroznor, Holder of the Laws... he will judge me. Please... say nothing to Woorwill. Let a trial call me guilty. Let me be the monster, not Rorworr. Tell Worrroznor, Holder of the Laws, in the home east of here.» [K1/Woorwill's_Home]
- Jaarak: «That's not for you to decide. You couldn't possibly understand how this affects my people or me.» [K1/Woorwill's_Home]

**[K1/Worrroznor's_Home]**
- Worrroznor: «I'm sorry. Like most of the village, I've been preoccupied by these troubled times. I don't really want to speak with you. Well, not unless you can tell me where young Rorworr is. Do you keep track of slaves taken?» [K1/Worrroznor's_Home]
- Worrroznor: «I have no way of knowing one way or another. You'll excuse me if I don't take you at your word.» [K1/Worrroznor's_Home]
- Worrroznor: «You could. I've never had an offer of help from an outsider. I'll reserve my judgment for when I see results.» [K1/Worrroznor's_Home]
- Worrroznor: «That would need to be determined. Wookiee justice rewards honor and honesty. You outsiders are a little lacking...» [K1/Worrroznor's_Home]
- Worrroznor: «Farewell then. Bacca keep you well.» [K1/Worrroznor's_Home]
- Worrroznor: «Hmm... let's see if the family insignia is there. Yes, there it is. This belongs to Jaarak. Er... how did you get it?» [K1/Worrroznor's_Home]
- Worrroznor: «That's the law. You discovered the crime, and therefore were also affected by it. You are also a victim. It's painful to know that another Wookiee could do wrong, so the accuser is repaid, as is the victim's family. This will be the first time an outsider has received such reparation. I hope it is the last.» [K1/Worrroznor's_Home]
- Woorwill: «What? What are you talking about? Liar!» [K1/Worrroznor's_Home]
- Worrroznor: «Explain yourself, outsider. If you have information that will affect this trial, I must know it.» [K1/Worrroznor's_Home]
- Worrroznor: «Did I not explain? As the person that discovered the crime, you were also affected by it. You would've been given a portion of Jaarak's possessions, but he was innocent. Rorworr, however, was not. Rorworr was wealthy. His family was taken by slavers, leaving him everything. You get one third of this. Woorwill receives the rest, if he wishes. He is still upset, but he is young. He will recover.» [K1/Worrroznor's_Home]
- Worrroznor: «Nothing that you can help with. Rorworr's crime is causing a bit of a stir, but it will pass in time. Some of us will be disappointed in him, but we have other enemies to worry about.» [K1/Worrroznor's_Home]
- Worrroznor: «Goodbye. Bacca guide you.» [K1/Worrroznor's_Home]
- Woorwill: «Nothing? There was no reason? You just killed him? Why! There has to be a reason why!» [K1/Worrroznor's_Home]
- Worrroznor: «And so he shall. Jaarak, you will be taken from us and cast into shadow. Your hunt ends.» [K1/Worrroznor's_Home]
- Worrroznor: «Yes, yes there is. You are to receive the credit equivalent of one third of Jaarak's possessions. Woorwill gets the rest. He is the closest to family that Rorworr had left. The others were lost to slavers. Here, take it. I thank you for your help. Now please, just go.» [K1/Worrroznor's_Home]
- Worrroznor: «A few details to work out following Jaarak's death, but nothing that need concern you.» [K1/Worrroznor's_Home]

**[K1/Yavin_Station]**
- Suvam Tan: «[Failure] Ha! Davik went down with Taris when the Sith destroyed it. That much I've learned from the local Exchange. So who are you?» [K1/Yavin_Station]
- Suvam Tan: «Republic, eh? The Republic hasn't been here in 50 years... don't see why they'd start now. Still, I have nothing against the Republic, so as long as you don't get all worked up, I guess I can let you in.» [K1/Yavin_Station]
- Suvam Tan: «They come by here every once in a while, it's how I keep in the loop, you know. But Trandoshan lizard-speak is kind of hard to follow and they have pretty limited imaginations... Smugglers aren't much better either...» [K1/Yavin_Station]
- Suvam Tan: «[Success] Yes... I'll open the door for you, friend.» [K1/Yavin_Station]
- Suvam Tam: «Yeah I know... Some damn smugglers must have brought them and one escaped. Good luck trying to get them all, though. Those things breed like... like gizka! If you see any around, just squish the damn thing!» [K1/Yavin_Station]
- Suvam Tam: «Eh? No need to be rude. I was just trying to be friendly is all... You don't have to bite my head off. If you don't want to talk, then I don't really know what you're doing here. There's nothing really here in this end of space anymore.» [K1/Yavin_Station]
- Suvam Tam: «Now, now, I never said I doubted you, it's just... well, there aren't exactly a lot of Jedi wandering around these parts, not after what happened last time.» [K1/Yavin_Station]
- Suvam Tam: «So if you don't mind saying, what exactly is the Council sending you out to do?» [K1/Yavin_Station]
- Suvam Tam: «Eh? No, no, of course not. A big (wo)man like you? No, no. But why are you here in the middle of nowhere? The ruins?» [K1/Yavin_Station]
- Suvam Tam: «Well, you see, I'm kind of out of touch with the rest of the galaxy. I haven't left this system in almost 50 years, and this station in the last five, so... Hey! It's not a bad way to live! I have all the gadgets I need to tinker with and the smugglers and Trandoshans bring me food and interesting pieces of equipment!» [K1/Yavin_Station]
